import { defineConfig } from 'vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import path from 'path';

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
  server: {
    port: 5173,
    // /api로 시작하는 요청을 스프링 부트(localhost:8080)로 전달
    proxy: {
      '/api': {
        target: 'http://localhost:8080',
        changeOrigin: true,
        secure: false,
      },
    },
  },
  build: {
    // npm run build 시 스프링의 static 리소스 폴더로 바로 산출물 생성
    outDir: path.resolve(__dirname, '../../src/main/resources/static'),
    emptyOutDir: true,
  },
});

import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route } from "react-router-dom";
import './App.css';
import { HomePage } from "./pages/HomePage.tsx";
import { SignupPage } from "./pages/SignupPage.tsx";
import { LoginPage } from "./pages/LoginPage.tsx";
import { KakaoCallback } from "./pages/KakaoCallback.tsx";
import { StayDetailPage } from "./pages/StayDetailPage.tsx";
import { ReservationPage } from "./pages/ReservationPage.tsx"; // 💡 추가

function App() {
    return (
        <BrowserRouter>
            <Routes>
                <Route path="/" element={<HomePage />} />
                <Route path="/signup" element={<SignupPage />} />
                <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
                <Route path="/oauth/callback" element={<KakaoCallback />} />
                <Route path="/stays/:contentId" element={<StayDetailPage />} />
                <Route path="/reservation" element={<ReservationPage />} /> {/* 💡 추가 */}
            </Routes>
        </BrowserRouter>
    );
}

export default App;

/**
 * [테이블 5] ACCOMMODATION + JPA DTO 응답 규격
 */
export type  Stay= {
  contentId: string;       // PK, 식별자 (기존 id 대신 사용)
  memberId?: number | null;
  title: string;           // 숙소명 (기존 name 대신 사용)
  lodgingType?: string;    // AC01 ~ AC06 코드 또는 숙박 유형
  tel?: string;
  zipcode?: string;
  addr?: string;           // 기본 주소 (기존 address 대신 사용)
  checkinTime?: string;
  checkoutTime?: string;
  roomCount?: string;
  roomType?: string;
  parking?: string;
  subFacility?: string;
  overview?: string;       // 숙소 개요
  firstImage?: string;     // 대표 이미지 URL
  firstImage2?: string;    // 서브 썸네일 URL
  mapX?: string;
  mapY?: string;
  basePrice: number;       // 객실 최저가 (원화)
};

export type Room = {
  roomId: number;
  contentId: string;
  roomTitle?: string;
  roomSize?: string;
  roomBaseCount?: string;
  roomMaxCount?: string;
  pricePerNight?: number;
};

export type Booking = {
  contentId: string;
  roomId?: number;
  checkIn: string;
  checkOut: string;
  guests: number;
  guestName: string;
  phone: string;
};

export type BookingResponse = {
  reservationCode?: string;
  id?: number;
  status?: string;
  message?: string;
};

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';
import { KakaoStayMap } from './KakaoMap';

interface StayMapModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    stays: Stay[];
}

export function StayMapModal({ isOpen, onClose, stays }: StayMapModalProps) {
    // 1. 모달 활성화 시 배경 스크롤 방지
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        } else {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        }
        return () => {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        };
    }, [isOpen]);

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                position: 'fixed',
                top: 0,
                left: 0,
                width: '100vw',
                height: '100vh',
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65)',
                backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                zIndex: 3000,
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                padding: '24px',
            }}
            onClick={onClose}
        >
            <div
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    maxWidth: '1100px',
                    height: '85vh',
                    maxHeight: '780px',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '24px',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'column',
                    boxShadow: '0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35)',
                    padding: '28px',
                    position: 'relative',
                    boxSizing: 'border-box',
                }}
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} // 내부 클릭 시 모달 닫힘 방지
            >
                {/* 닫기 (X) 버튼 */}
                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onClose}
                    aria-label="닫기"
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        top: '24px',
                        right: '24px',
                        background: '#f1f5f9',
                        border: 'none',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        color: '#64748b',
                        width: '36px',
                        height: '36px',
                        borderRadius: '50%',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        transition: 'background-color 0.2s',
                        zIndex: 10,
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#e2e8f0')}
                    onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#f1f5f9')}
                >
                    <X size={20} />
                </button>

                {/* 2. 기존 홈페이지 section-top 헤더 영역 */}
                <div
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'flex-end',
                        marginBottom: '18px',
                        paddingRight: '48px', // X 버튼과 겹치지 않도록 여백
                    }}
                >
                    <div>
                        <span
                            className="eyebrow"
                            style={{
                                color: '#b45309',
                                fontSize: '12px',
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                letterSpacing: '0.05em',
                                display: 'block',
                                marginBottom: '4px',
                            }}
                        >
                            JEJU MAP GUIDE
                        </span>
                        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>
                            제주, 어디로 갈까요?
                        </h2>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: 0 }}>
                            마커나 클러스터를 클릭하면 숙소 정보와 1박 요금을 확인하고 바로 이동할 수 있습니다.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <span
                        style={{
                            color: '#0f766e',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            backgroundColor: '#ccfbf1',
                            padding: '6px 14px',
                            borderRadius: '20px',
                            fontSize: '12px',
                            whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                        }}
                    >
                        실시간 등록 숙소 {stays.length}곳 탐색 가능
                    </span>
                </div>

                {/* 3. 지도 래퍼 카드 (모달 남은 높이를 꽉 채우도록 설정) */}
                <div
                    style={{
                        flex: 1,
                        minHeight: 0,
                        borderRadius: '18px',
                        overflow: 'hidden',
                        boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08)',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        position: 'relative',
                    }}
                >
                    <KakaoStayMap stays={stays} level={9} height="100%" />
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}
========================================
File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/components/KakaoMap.tsx
========================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';
import { money, getLodgingTypeName } from '../data/stays';

declare const window: typeof globalThis & {
    kakao: any;
};

interface KakaoStayMapProps {
    stays: Stay[];
    level?: number;
    height?: string;
    mode?: 'search' | 'detail'; // 💡 모드 구분 (기본값: 'search')
}

const KAKAO_MAP_KEY = '1eb6f2a2617e0c3b3bb7eb7b90331bae';

export function KakaoStayMap({ stays, level, height = '520px', mode = 'search' }: KakaoStayMapProps) {
    const mapContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [isLoaded, setIsLoaded] = useState(false);

    // 1. 카카오 지도 SDK 동적 주입
    useEffect(() => {
        const existingScript = document.getElementById('kakao-map-sdk');
        if (existingScript) {
            if (window.kakao && window.kakao.maps) {
                setIsLoaded(true);
            } else {
                existingScript.addEventListener('load', () => setIsLoaded(true));
            }
            return;
        }

        const script = document.createElement('script');
        script.id = 'kakao-map-sdk';
        script.src = `//dapi.kakao.com/v2/maps/sdk.js?appkey=${KAKAO_MAP_KEY}&libraries=clusterer&autoload=false`;
        script.async = true;

        script.onload = () => {
            setIsLoaded(true);
        };

        document.head.appendChild(script);
    }, []);

    // 2. 지도 초기화 및 마커 렌더링
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isLoaded || !window.kakao || !window.kakao.maps) return;

        window.kakao.maps.load(() => {
            if (!mapContainerRef.current) return;

            const validStays = stays.filter(
                (s) => s.mapX && s.mapY && !isNaN(Number(s.mapX)) && !isNaN(Number(s.mapY))
            );

            // 💡 상세페이지 모드인지 여부 (mode가 'detail'일 때만 작동)
            const isDetailPage = mode === 'detail';
            const isSingleStay = validStays.length === 1;

            // 중심 좌표: 상세페이지이거나 검색 결과가 딱 1곳이면 해당 숙소 좌표, 그 외는 제주도 전역
            const centerPosition = (isDetailPage || isSingleStay) && validStays.length > 0
                ? new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(
                    parseFloat(validStays[0].mapY!),
                    parseFloat(validStays[0].mapX!)
                )
                : new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(33.3617, 126.5292);

            // 줌 레벨: level prop 우선 -> 상세페이지면 4(근접), 검색 모달이면 숙소 1개여도 적당히 주변이 보이는 6~7 또는 지정된 레벨
            const zoomLevel = level ?? (isDetailPage ? 4 : isSingleStay ? 6 : 9);

            const options = {
                center: centerPosition,
                level: zoomLevel,
            };
            const map = new window.kakao.maps.Map(mapContainerRef.current, options);

            // =========================================================
            // 💡 [케이스 A] 객실 상세페이지 전용 뷰 (mode === 'detail')
            // : 숙박종류 + 숙소명 + 상세 주소 + 주소 복사 버튼 (가격/상세보기 제외)
            // =========================================================
            if (isDetailPage && validStays.length > 0) {
                const stay = validStays[0];
                const marker = new window.kakao.maps.Marker({
                    position: centerPosition,
                    map: map,
                });

                const detailCard = document.createElement('div');
                detailCard.style.cssText = `
                    background: #ffffff;
                    border-radius: 12px;
                    box-shadow: 0 4px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16);
                    border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                    padding: 12px 14px;
                    min-width: 220px;
                    max-width: 280px;
                    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
                    position: relative;
                `;

                // 💡 상단 X(닫기) 버튼 및 주소 복사 버튼 추가
                detailCard.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                        <span style="font-size: 11px; color: #0f766e; font-weight: 700;">
                            ${getLodgingTypeName(stay.lodgingType)}
                        </span>
                        <button id="btn-close-detail-overlay" style="
                            background: none;
                            border: none;
                            font-size: 14px;
                            color: #94a3b8;
                            cursor: pointer;
                            padding: 0 0 0 8px;
                            line-height: 1;
                        ">✕</button>
                    </div>
                    <strong style="display: block; font-size: 14px; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px; line-height: 1.3;">
                        ${stay.title}
                    </strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.4; margin-bottom: 8px; word-break: keep-all;">
                        ${stay.addr || '등록된 주소 정보 없음'}
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                        <button id="btn-copy-stay-addr" style="
                            flex: 1;
                            padding: 6px 0;
                            background-color: #f1f5f9;
                            color: #123f4d;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 11px;
                            font-weight: 600;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            주소 복사
                        </button>
                        <button id="btn-close-bottom" style="
                            padding: 6px 12px;
                            background-color: #ffffff;
                            color: #64748b;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 11px;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            닫기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const overlay = new window.kakao.maps.CustomOverlay({
                    content: detailCard,
                    position: centerPosition,
                    yAnchor: 1.3,
                });

                // 처음에는 열어둠
                overlay.setMap(map);

                // 마커 클릭 시 다시 열기
                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(marker, 'click', () => {
                    overlay.setMap(map);
                });

                // 주소 복사 및 닫기 버튼 이벤트 연결
                setTimeout(() => {
                    const copyBtn = document.getElementById('btn-copy-stay-addr');
                    const closeXBtn = document.getElementById('btn-close-detail-overlay');
                    const closeBottomBtn = document.getElementById('btn-close-bottom');

                    if (copyBtn && stay.addr) {
                        copyBtn.onclick = () => {
                            navigator.clipboard.writeText(stay.addr as string);
                            alert('주소가 클립보드에 복사되었습니다.');
                        };
                    }
                    if (closeXBtn) {
                        closeXBtn.onclick = () => overlay.setMap(null);
                    }
                    if (closeBottomBtn) {
                        closeBottomBtn.onclick = () => overlay.setMap(null);
                    }
                }, 0);

                return;
            }

            // =========================================================
            // 💡 [케이스 B] 검색/홈페이지 지도 모드 (mode === 'search')
            // : 숙소가 1개든 여러 개든 숙박종류 + 이름 + 주소 + 가격 + 상세보기 버튼 전체 노출!
            // =========================================================
            const clusterer = new window.kakao.maps.MarkerClusterer({
                map: map,
                averageCenter: true,
                minLevel: 6,
                disableClickZoom: false,
            });

            let currentOverlay: any = null;

            const markers = validStays.map((stay) => {
                const lat = parseFloat(stay.mapY!);
                const lng = parseFloat(stay.mapX!);
                const position = new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(lat, lng);

                const marker = new window.kakao.maps.Marker({
                    position: position,
                });

                // 카드형 오버레이 UI 엘리먼트 생성
                const content = document.createElement('div');
                content.style.cssText = `
                    background: #ffffff;
                    border-radius: 14px;
                    box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.18);
                    border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                    padding: 14px 16px;
                    width: 230px;
                    position: relative;
                    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
                `;

                content.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #0f766e; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                        ${getLodgingTypeName(stay.lodgingType)}
                    </div>
                    <strong style="display: block; font-size: 14px; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
                        ${stay.title}
                    </strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.3; margin-bottom: 8px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
                        ${stay.addr || ''}
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; color: #123f4d; margin-bottom: 10px;">
                        ${money(stay.basePrice)} <span style="font-size: 11px; font-weight: 400; color: #94a3b8;">/ 1박</span>
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                        <button id="btn-detail-${stay.contentId}" style="
                            flex: 1;
                            padding: 7px 0;
                            background-color: #123f4d;
                            color: #ffffff;
                            border: none;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 12px;
                            font-weight: 600;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            상세보기
                        </button>
                        <button id="btn-close-${stay.contentId}" style="
                            padding: 7px 10px;
                            background-color: #f1f5f9;
                            color: #475569;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 12px;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            닫기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const overlay = new window.kakao.maps.CustomOverlay({
                    content: content,
                    position: position,
                    yAnchor: 1.25,
                });

                const showOverlay = () => {
                    if (currentOverlay) {
                        currentOverlay.setMap(null);
                    }
                    overlay.setMap(map);
                    currentOverlay = overlay;

                    setTimeout(() => {
                        const detailBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-detail-${stay.contentId}`);
                        const closeBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-close-${stay.contentId}`);

                        if (detailBtn) {
                            detailBtn.onclick = () => navigate(`/stays/${stay.contentId}`);
                        }
                        if (closeBtn) {
                            closeBtn.onclick = () => overlay.setMap(null);
                        }
                    }, 0);
                };

                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(marker, 'click', showOverlay);

                // 💡 만약 검색 결과가 딱 1곳뿐이라면 클릭하지 않아도 카드 오버레이를 자동으로 즉시 열어둠!
                if (isSingleStay) {
                    showOverlay();
                }

                return marker;
            });

            // 검색 결과가 1개일 땐 마커를 지도에 직접 찍고, 2개 이상일 땐 클러스터러 적용
            if (isSingleStay) {
                markers[0].setMap(map);
            } else {
                clusterer.addMarkers(markers);
            }
        });
    }, [isLoaded, stays, level, mode, navigate]);

    return (
        <div
            ref={mapContainerRef}
            style={{
                width: '100%',
                height: height,
                borderRadius: '16px',
                border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                overflow: 'hidden',
                boxShadow: '0 2px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)',
                backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
            }}
        >
            {!isLoaded && (
                <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>
                    지도를 불러오는 중입니다...
                </span>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}
========================================
File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/components/BookingModal.tsx

import { ArrowRight, MapPin, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { money, getLodgingTypeName } from '../data/stays';
import type { useReservation } from '../hooks/useReservation';

type Reservation = ReturnType<typeof useReservation>;

export function BookingModal({ reservation }: { reservation: Reservation }) {
  const { selected, close } = reservation;
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  if (!selected) return null;

  const bgImage = selected.firstImage || selected.firstImage2 || '/jeju-stays.png';

  const handleGoDetail = () => {
    close();
    navigate(`/stays/${selected.contentId}`);
  };

  return (
      <div className="modal-backdrop" onMouseDown={(e) => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) close(); }}>
        <section className="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="booking-title" style={{ maxWidth: '480px' }}>
          <button className="modal-close" onClick={close} aria-label="닫기">
            <X size={21} />
          </button>
          <div className="modal-image crop-0" style={{ backgroundImage: `url(${bgImage})`, height: '220px' }} />
          <div className="modal-body" style={{ padding: '24px' }}>
            <span className="eyebrow">JEJU STAY</span>
            <h2 id="booking-title" style={{ fontSize: '22px', margin: '4px 0 10px 0' }}>{selected.title}</h2>
            <p className="modal-address" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px', color: '#64748b', fontSize: '13px' }}>
              <MapPin size={16} />
              {selected.addr} · {getLodgingTypeName(selected.lodgingType)}
            </p>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '14px', color: '#475569', lineHeight: 1.6, margin: '14px 0' }}>
              {selected.overview ? `${selected.overview.slice(0, 110)}...` : '아늑하고 편안한 제주 숙소입니다.'}
            </p>
            <div className="modal-price" style={{ margin: '16px 0 20px 0', padding: '12px 16px', background: '#f8fafc', borderRadius: '8px' }}>
              1박 최저가 <strong style={{ color: '#0f766e', fontSize: '18px', marginLeft: '6px' }}>{money(selected.basePrice)}</strong>
            </div>

            <button
                type="button"
                className="reserve-button"
                onClick={handleGoDetail}
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  padding: '14px',
                  backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                  color: '#ffffff',
                  border: 'none',
                  borderRadius: '8px',
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  fontSize: '15px',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  gap: '8px'
                }}
            >
              숙소 상세 및 객실 보러가기 <ArrowRight size={17} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Bell, X } from 'lucide-react';

interface NotificationItem {
    id: string;
    title: string;
    message: string;
    type: string;
    createdAt: string;
}

interface NotificationBellProps {
    userRole: string;
    isLoggedIn: boolean;
}

export function NotificationBell({ userRole, isLoggedIn }: NotificationBellProps) {
    const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<NotificationItem[]>([]);
    const [isNotiOpen, setIsNotiOpen] = useState(false);
    const notiRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    // 알림 목록 조회 (ROLE_HOST 및 ROLE_ADMIN 전용)
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isLoggedIn && (userRole === 'ROLE_HOST' || userRole === 'ROLE_ADMIN')) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
            fetch('/api/v1/notifications', {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            })
                .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : []))
                .then((data: NotificationItem[]) => {
                    const dismissed: string[] = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dismissedNotiIds') || '[]');
                    const filtered = data.filter((item) => !dismissed.includes(item.id));
                    setNotifications(filtered);
                })
                .catch(() => setNotifications([]));
        }
    }, [isLoggedIn, userRole]);

    // 알림창 바깥 클릭 시 닫기
    useEffect(() => {
        const handleClickOutsideNoti = (event: MouseEvent) => {
            if (notiRef.current && !notiRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
                setIsNotiOpen(false);
            }
        };
        document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutsideNoti);
        return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutsideNoti);
    }, []);

    // X 버튼 클릭 시 로컬 삭제 처리
    const handleDeleteNoti = (id: string) => {
        const dismissed: string[] = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dismissedNotiIds') || '[]');
        if (!dismissed.includes(id)) {
            dismissed.push(id);
            localStorage.setItem('dismissedNotiIds', JSON.stringify(dismissed));
        }
        setNotifications((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
    };

    // 호스트나 관리자가 아니면 아무것도 렌더링하지 않음
    if (!isLoggedIn || (userRole !== 'ROLE_HOST' && userRole !== 'ROLE_ADMIN')) {
        return null;
    }

    return (
        <div style={{ position: 'relative' }} ref={notiRef}>
            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsNotiOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                style={{
                    position: 'relative',
                    width: '38px',
                    height: '38px',
                    borderRadius: '50%',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    border: '1.5px solid #123f4d',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    color: '#123f4d'
                }}
                aria-label="알림"
            >
                <Bell size={18} />

                {notifications.length > 0 && (
                    <span
                        style={{
                            position: 'absolute',
                            top: '3px',
                            left: '3px',
                            width: '8px',
                            height: '8px',
                            backgroundColor: '#e53e3e',
                            borderRadius: '50%',
                            border: '1.5px solid #ffffff'
                        }}
                    />
                )}
            </button>

            {isNotiOpen && (
                <div
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        top: '115%',
                        right: 0,
                        width: '300px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        boxShadow: '0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        padding: '12px',
                        zIndex: 1100
                    }}
                >
                    <div
                        style={{
                            display: 'flex',
                            justifyContent: 'space-between',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            marginBottom: '8px',
                            paddingBottom: '6px',
                            borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9'
                        }}
                    >
                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#1e293b' }}>알림 내역</strong>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#64748b' }}>{notifications.length}건</span>
                    </div>

                    {notifications.length === 0 ? (
                        <div style={{ padding: '20px 0', textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                            새로운 알림이 없습니다.
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px', maxHeight: '250px', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
                            {notifications.map((item) => (
                                <div
                                    key={item.id}
                                    style={{
                                        display: 'flex',
                                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                        alignItems: 'flex-start',
                                        backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                                        padding: '8px 10px',
                                        borderRadius: '8px',
                                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                        gap: '8px'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <div>
                                        <p
                                            style={{
                                                margin: '0 0 3px 0',
                                                fontSize: '12px',
                                                fontWeight: 600,
                                                color: item.type === 'ACCOM_REJECT' ? '#e53e3e' : '#0f766e'
                                            }}
                                        >
                                            {item.title}
                                        </p>
                                        <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '11px', color: '#475569', wordBreak: 'break-word', lineHeight: '1.4' }}>
                                            {item.message}
                                        </p>
                                    </div>
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={() => handleDeleteNoti(item.id)}
                                        style={{
                                            background: 'none',
                                            border: 'none',
                                            cursor: 'pointer',
                                            padding: '2px',
                                            color: '#94a3b8'
                                        }}
                                        aria-label="알림 지우기"
                                    >
                                        <X size={14} />
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { requestApplyHost } from '../api/auth';
import axios from 'axios';
import { X, Building } from 'lucide-react';

export function HostApplyBanner() {
    const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [businessNo, setBusinessNo] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (token) {
            try {
                const base64Payload = token.split('.')[1];
                const decodedPayload = JSON.parse(
                    decodeURIComponent(
                        atob(base64Payload)
                            .split('')
                            .map((c) => '%' + ('00' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16)).slice(-2))
                            .join('')
                    )
                );
                // 토큰에서 역할 추출
                setUserRole(decodedPayload.role || null);
            } catch {
                setUserRole(null);
            }
        } else {
            setUserRole(null);
        }
    }, []);

    // 💡 로그인하지 않았거나, 일반 사용자(ROLE_USER)가 아니면 화면에 아예 그리지 않음
    if (userRole !== 'ROLE_USER') {
        return null;
    }

    const handleApply = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!businessNo.trim()) {
            setErrorMessage('사업자등록번호를 입력해주세요.');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);
        setErrorMessage('');

        try {
            const data = await requestApplyHost(businessNo.trim());
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', data.token);
            alert('숙소 관리자 신청이 완료되었습니다! 파트너 권한으로 전환됩니다.');
            setIsModalOpen(false);
            window.location.reload();
        } catch (err: unknown) {
            if (axios.isAxiosError(err) && err.response) {
                setErrorMessage(err.response.data?.message || '신청 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
            } else {
                setErrorMessage('서버와 통신할 수 없습니다.');
            }
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (

        // 숙소 관리자 신청
        <section style={{ backgroundColor: '#0f333f', padding: '24px 0', borderTop: '1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)' }}>
            <div className="container" style={{
                display: 'grid',
                gridTemplateColumns: '1fr auto 1fr', // 💡 좌(비움) : 중앙(문구) : 우(버튼) 3분할
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '16px'
            }}>
                {/* 1. 좌측 균형을 맞추기 위한 빈 영역 */}
                <div />

                {/* 2. 중앙으로 배치되는 문구 영역 */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                    <div style={{
                        width: '36px',
                        height: '36px',
                        borderRadius: '8px',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(110, 231, 183, 0.15)',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center'
                    }}>
                        <Building size={20} color="#6ee7b7" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <strong style={{ display: 'block', color: '#ffffff', fontSize: '15px' }}>
                            숙소를 신청하고 싶은가요?
                        </strong>
                        <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                파트너로 등록하고 더 많은 여행객에게 숙소를 소개해 보세요.
            </span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 3. 우측 끝에 고정되는 버튼 영역 */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(true)}
                        style={{
                            padding: '10px 22px',
                            fontSize: '14px',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            color: '#123f4d',
                            backgroundColor: '#6ee7b7',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderRadius: '24px',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(110, 231, 183, 0.2)',
                        }}
                    >
                        숙소관리자 신청
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* 사업자번호 입력 모달창 */}
            {isModalOpen && (
                <div style={{
                    position: 'fixed',
                    top: 0,
                    left: 0,
                    width: '100vw',
                    height: '100vh',
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55)',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    zIndex: 2000
                }}>
                    <div style={{
                        width: '90%',
                        maxWidth: '400px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '16px',
                        padding: '28px',
                        boxShadow: '0 20px 25px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2)',
                        position: 'relative'
                    }}>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                            style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '18px', right: '18px', background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                        >
                            <X size={20} color="#64748b" />
                        </button>

                        <h3 style={{ margin: '0 0 8px 0', fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a' }}>숙소 관리자(호스트) 신청</h3>
                        <p style={{ margin: '0 0 20px 0', fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                            운영 중이신 사업자등록번호를 입력하시면 즉시 호스트 권한이 부여됩니다.
                        </p>

                        {errorMessage && (
                            <div style={{ padding: '8px 12px', backgroundColor: '#fee2e2', color: '#dc2626', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '12px', marginBottom: '14px' }}>
                                {errorMessage}
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <form onSubmit={handleApply}>
                            <div style={{ marginBottom: '18px' }}>
                                <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#334155', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                    사업자등록번호
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    required
                                    value={businessNo}
                                    onChange={(e) => setBusinessNo(e.target.value)}
                                    placeholder="000-00-00000"
                                    style={{
                                        width: '100%',
                                        padding: '10px 12px',
                                        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                        borderRadius: '8px',
                                        boxSizing: 'border-box',
                                        fontSize: '14px',
                                    }}
                                />
                            </div>

                            <button
                                type="submit"
                                disabled={loading}
                                style={{
                                    width: '100%',
                                    padding: '11px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    fontWeight: 600,
                                    fontSize: '14px',
                                    cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer'
                                }}
                            >
                                {loading ? '신청 진행 중...' : '신청 완료'}
                            </button>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </section>
    );
}

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
//해당 컴포넌트는 예제용으로 차후 삭제 요망
interface ApiResponse {
    status: string;
    message: string;
    port: number;
}

export default function ApiTest() {
    const [data, setData] = useState<ApiResponse | null>(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('/api/hello')
            .then((res) => {
                if (!res.ok) {
                    throw new Error(`HTTP 에러 발생: ${res.status}`);
                }
                return res.json();
            })
            .then((json: ApiResponse) => {
                setData(json);
                setLoading(false);
            })
            .catch((err) => {
                setError(err.message);
                setLoading(false);
            });
    }, []);

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                margin: '20px auto',
                maxWidth: '600px',
                padding: '20px',
                border: '1px solid #4caf50',
                borderRadius: '8px',
                backgroundColor: '#f1f8e9',
                fontFamily: 'sans-serif',
            }}
        >
            <h3 style={{ margin: '0 0 10px 0', color: '#2e7d32' }}>
                🔗 Spring Boot & React 연동 상태
            </h3>

            {loading && <p>데이터를 불러오는 중입니다...</p>}
            {error && <p style={{ color: '#d32f2f' }}>연동 오류: {error}</p>}

            {data && (
                <div style={{ lineHeight: '1.6' }}>
                    <p style={{ margin: '4px 0' }}><strong>상태:</strong> {data.status}</p>
                    <p style={{ margin: '4px 0' }}><strong>서버 메시지:</strong> {data.message}</p>
                    <p style={{ margin: '4px 0' }}><strong>응답 포트:</strong> {data.port}</p>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/hooks/useReservation.ts

import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { createReservation } from '../api/stays';
import type { Booking, Stay } from '../types/stay';

export function useReservation(apiOnline: boolean) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Stay | null>(null);
  const [checkIn, setCheckIn] = useState('');
  const [checkOut, setCheckOut] = useState('');
  const [guests, setGuests] = useState(2);
  const [guestName, setGuestName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.style.overflow = selected ? 'hidden' : '';
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = ''; };
  }, [selected]);

  const open = (stay: Stay) => { setSelected(stay); setFeedback(''); };
  const close = () => { setSelected(null); setFeedback(''); };

  const book = async (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!selected) return;
    if (checkOut <= checkIn) {
      setFeedback('퇴실일은 입실일 다음 날부터 선택해 주세요.');
      return;
    }
    if (!apiOnline) {
      setFeedback('현재 예약 접수를 준비 중입니다.');
      return;
    }
    setSubmitting(true);
    setFeedback('');
    try {
      const booking: Booking = {
        contentId: selected.contentId, // 수정: contentId 바인딩
        checkIn,
        checkOut,
        guests,
        guestName,
        phone
      };
      const data = await createReservation(booking);
      setFeedback(`예약 요청이 접수되었습니다. 예약번호: ${data.reservationCode || data.id}`);
    } catch (error) {
      setFeedback(error instanceof Error ? error.message : '잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return { selected, open, close, checkIn, setCheckIn, checkOut, setCheckOut, guests, setGuests,
    guestName, setGuestName, phone, setPhone, submitting, feedback, book };
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/hooks/useHeroSlider.ts

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

const slideCount = 3;

export function useHeroSlider() {
  const [slide, setSlide] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setSlide(current => (current + 1) % slideCount), 10000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);
  const previous = () => setSlide(current => (current + slideCount - 1) % slideCount);
  const next = () => setSlide(current => (current + 1) % slideCount);
  return { slide, previous, next };
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { fetchStays } from '../data/stays';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';

export function useStays() {
  const [region, setRegion] = useState('전체 지역');
  const [type, setType] = useState('전체 숙박');
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [term, setTerm] = useState('');
  const [stays, setStays] = useState<Stay[]>([]);
  const [apiOnline, setApiOnline] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // 선택된 지역, 유형, 검색어가 바뀔 때마다 스프링 API 호출
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setLoading(true);

    fetchStays(region, type, term)
        .then(data => {
          if (active) {
            setStays(data);
            setApiOnline(true);
          }
        })
        .catch((err) => {
          console.error('숙소 로딩 실패:', err);
          if (active) setApiOnline(false);
        })
        .finally(() => {
          if (active) setLoading(false);
        });

    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [region, type, term]);

  const selectRegion = (value: string) => {
    setRegion(value);
    document.getElementById('stays')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
  };

  const search = () => {
    setTerm(query);
    document.getElementById('stays')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  return {
    region,
    setRegion,
    type,
    setType,
    query,
    setQuery,
    result: stays,
    loading,
    apiOnline,
    selectRegion,
    search,
  };
}
========================================
File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/api/stays.ts

import type { Booking, BookingResponse, Stay } from '../types/stay';

const apiBase = (import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || '').replace(/\/$/, '');

export async function fetchStays(): Promise<Stay[]> {
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/v1/stays`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('숙소 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
  const stays: unknown = await response.json();
  if (!Array.isArray(stays)) throw new Error('숙소 응답 형식이 올바르지 않습니다.');
  return stays as Stay[];
}

export async function createReservation(booking: Booking): Promise<BookingResponse> {
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/v1/reservations`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(booking),
  });
  const data: BookingResponse = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.message || '예약 요청을 처리하지 못했습니다.');
  return data;
}

import axios from 'axios';

export interface SignupPayload {
    email: string;
    password: string;
    name: string;
    businessNo?: string | null;
}

export interface LoginPayload {
    email: string;
    password: string;
}

export interface TokenResponse {
    token: string;
}

// 회원가입
export const requestSignup = async (payload: SignupPayload): Promise<string> => {
    const response = await axios.post<string>('/api/v1/auth/signup', payload);
    return response.data;
};

// 일반 로그인
export const requestLogin = async (payload: LoginPayload): Promise<TokenResponse> => {
    const response = await axios.post<TokenResponse>('/api/v1/auth/login', payload);
    return response.data;
};

// 카카오 로그인 인증창 URL
const KAKAO_CLIENT_ID = '9c6e519edc47e02f6460edd2afd70d35';
const KAKAO_REDIRECT_URI = 'http://localhost:8080/api/v1/auth/kakao/callback';

export const KAKAO_AUTH_URL = `https://kauth.kakao.com/oauth/authorize?client_id=${KAKAO_CLIENT_ID}&redirect_uri=${KAKAO_REDIRECT_URI}&response_type=code&prompt=login`;
// 숙소 관리자(호스트) 신청 API
export const requestApplyHost = async (businessNo: string): Promise<TokenResponse> => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
    const response = await axios.post<TokenResponse>(
        '/api/v1/auth/apply-host',
        {businessNo},
        {
            headers: {
                Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
            },
        }
    );
    return response.data;
};

import axios from 'axios';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';

// 1. 지역 필터
export const regions = [
 '전체 지역',
 '제주시',
 '애월·한림',
 '함덕·구좌',
 '서귀포·중문',
 '성산·표선',
] as const;

// 2. 숙박 유형 필터
export const types = [
 '전체 숙박',
 '호텔',
 '콘도/리조트',
 '펜션/풀빌라',
 '모텔',
 '캠핑/글램핑',
 '게스트하우스/호스텔',
] as const;

// 3. 메인 추천 여행지 배너
export const destinations = [
 { name: '애월·한림', note: '해안도로와 석양', className: 'spot-a' },
 { name: '서귀포·중문', note: '폭포와 남쪽 바다', className: 'spot-b' },
 { name: '성산·표선', note: '일출과 오름', className: 'spot-c' },
];

// 4. 금액 포맷팅 헬퍼
export const money = (n?: number) => {
 if (!n || n <= 0) return '요금 문의';
 return new Intl.NumberFormat('ko-KR').format(n) + '원';
};

// 5. DB 코드(AC01~AC06)를 한글 숙박 유형명으로 변환
export const getLodgingTypeName = (code?: string): string => {
 switch (code) {
  case 'AC01': return '호텔';
  case 'AC02': return '콘도미니엄';
  case 'AC03': return '펜션/민박';
  case 'AC04': return '모텔';
  case 'AC05': return '캠핑';
  case 'AC06': return '호스텔';
  default: return code || '숙소';
 }
};

// 6. 지역 필터명을 백엔드 검색 키워드로 매핑
export const mapRegionToKeyword = (region: string): string => {
 if (region === '전체 지역' || !region) return '';
 if (region === '애월·한림') return '애월';
 if (region === '함덕·구좌') return '구좌';
 if (region === '서귀포·중문') return '서귀포';
 if (region === '성산·표선') return '성산';
 return region; // '제주시' 등
};

// 7. 숙박 유형명을 백엔드 파라미터(AC01~AC06)로 매핑
export const mapTypeToKeyword = (type: string): string => {
 if (!type || type === '전체 숙박') return '';
 if (type === '호텔') return 'AC01';
 if (type.includes('콘도') || type.includes('리조트')) return 'AC02';
 if (type.includes('펜션')) return 'AC03';
 if (type.includes('모텔')) return 'AC04';
 if (type.includes('캠핑')) return 'AC05';
 if (type.includes('호스텔') || type.includes('게스트하우스')) return 'AC06';
 return type;
};

// 8. 백엔드 숙소 목록 비동기 조회 함수
export const fetchStays = async (
    region?: string,
    type?: string,
    query?: string
): Promise<Stay[]> => {
 const params: Record<string, string> = {};

 const regionKeyword = mapRegionToKeyword(region || '');
 if (regionKeyword) params.region = regionKeyword;

 const typeKeyword = mapTypeToKeyword(type || '');
 if (typeKeyword) params.type = typeKeyword;

 if (query && query.trim()) params.query = query.trim();

 const response = await axios.get<Stay[]>('/api/v1/stays', { params });
 return response.data;
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { requestLogin, KAKAO_AUTH_URL } from '../api/auth';
import axios from 'axios';
import {Footer} from "../components/Footer.tsx";

export function LoginPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');

    const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setErrorMessage('');
        setLoading(true);

        try {
            const data = await requestLogin({ email, password });
            // JWT 토큰 저장
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', data.token);
            alert('로그인되었습니다!');
            navigate('/');
        } catch (err: unknown) {
            if (axios.isAxiosError(err) && err.response) {
                setErrorMessage(err.response.data?.message || '이메일 또는 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
            } else {
                setErrorMessage('서버와 통신할 수 없습니다.');
            }
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    const handleKakaoLogin = () => {
        window.location.href = KAKAO_AUTH_URL;
    };

    return (
        <>
        <div style={{ maxWidth: '420px', margin: '60px auto', padding: '32px', border: '1px solid #e0e0e0', borderRadius: '12px', backgroundColor: '#fff' }}>
            <h2 style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: '8px', color: '#123f4d' }}>로그인</h2>
            <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#666', fontSize: '14px', marginBottom: '24px' }}>제주 야호 서비스를 이용해보세요.</p>

            {/* 카카오 로그인 버튼 */}
            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleKakaoLogin}
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    padding: '12px',
                    backgroundColor: '#FEE500',
                    color: '#000000',
                    border: 'none',
                    borderRadius: '6px',
                    fontWeight: 600,
                    fontSize: '14px',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    gap: '8px',
                    marginBottom: '20px'
                }}
            >
                카카오로 시작하기
            </button>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', margin: '16px 0', color: '#aaa', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#eee' }} />
                <span style={{ padding: '0 10px' }}>또는 이메일로 로그인</span>
                <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#eee' }} />
            </div>

            {errorMessage && (
                <div style={{ padding: '10px 14px', backgroundColor: '#fde8e8', color: '#e02424', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '13px', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                    {errorMessage}
                </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleLogin} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px' }}>
                <div>
                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px' }}>이메일</label>
                    <input
                        type="email"
                        required
                        value={email}
                        onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="example@jeju.com"
                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', border: '1px solid #ccc', borderRadius: '6px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                    />
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px' }}>비밀번호</label>
                    <input
                        type="password"
                        required
                        value={password}
                        onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="비밀번호 입력"
                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', border: '1px solid #ccc', borderRadius: '6px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                    />
                </div>

                <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={loading}
                    style={{
                        marginTop: '10px',
                        padding: '12px',
                        backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                        color: '#fff',
                        border: 'none',
                        borderRadius: '6px',
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                    }}
                >
                    {loading ? '로그인 중...' : '로그인'}
                </button>
            </form>

            <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '20px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#666' }}>
                아직 계정이 없으신가요? <Link to="/signup" style={{ color: '#123f4d', fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: 'none' }}>회원가입</Link>
            </div>
        </div>
            <Footer/>
        </>
    );
}

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';

export function KakaoCallback() {
    const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    useEffect(() => {
        const token = searchParams.get('token');
        if (token) {
            // 발급된 JWT 토큰을 로컬스토리지에 저장
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', token);
            alert('카카오 로그인에 성공했습니다!');
            navigate('/'); // 메인 화면으로 이동
        } else {
            alert('로그인에 실패했습니다.');
            navigate('/login');
        }
    }, [searchParams, navigate]);

    return (
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '100px', fontSize: '18px' }}>
            로그인 처리 중입니다. 잠시만 기다려주세요...
        </div>
    );
}

interface RoomDto {
    roomId: number;
    roomTitle: string;
    roomSize: string;

interface StayDetail {
    contentId: string;
    title: string;
    lodgingType: string;
    tel: string;
    zipcode: string;
    addr: string;
    checkinTime: string;
    checkoutTime: string;

    firstImage2: string;

}


export function StayDetailPage() {
    const {contentId} = useParams<{ contentId: string }>();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [stay, setStay] = useState<StayDetail | null>(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [isGalleryOpen, setIsGalleryOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isSellerInfoOpen, setIsSellerInfoOpen] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        axios.get<StayDetail>(`/api/v1/stays/${contentId}`)
            .then((res) => setStay(res.data))
            .catch((err) => {
                console.error(err);
                alert('숙소 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
                navigate('/');
            })
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, [contentId, navigate]);

    // 모달 활성화 시 배경 스크롤 방지
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isGalleryOpen) {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        } else {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        }
        return () => {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        };
    }, [isGalleryOpen]);

    // 숙소 대표 이미지 + 객실들의 모든 사진을 중복 없이 수집
    const allGalleryImages = useMemo(() => {
        if (!stay) return [];
        const set = new Set<string>();

        if (stay.firstImage) set.add(stay.firstImage);

        if (stay.rooms) {
            stay.rooms.forEach((r) => {
                if (r.imageUrls && r.imageUrls.length > 0) {
                    r.imageUrls.forEach((url) => {
                        if (url) set.add(url);
                    });
                }
            });
        }
        return Array.from(set);
    }, [stay]);

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div style={{textAlign: 'center', padding: '120px 0', color: '#64748b'}}>
                숙소 상세 정보를 불러오는 중입니다...
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (!stay) return null;

    const facilities = stay.subFacility ? stay.subFacility.split(',').map((f) => f.trim()) : [];

    // 상단 5분할 그리드에 노출할 5개 이미지 추출
    const mainImages = allGalleryImages.slice(0, 5);

    return (
        <div style={{backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh'}}>
            {/* 상단 네비게이션 헤더 */}

                backgroundColor: '#ffffff',

                position: 'sticky',
                top: 0,
                zIndex: 100
            }}>
                <div className="container" style={{height: '60px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '16px'}}>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => navigate(-1)}
                        style={{
                            background: 'none',
                            border: 'none',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center'
                        }}
                    >
                        <ChevronLeft size={24} color="#123f4d"/>
                    </button>
                    <span style={{fontSize: '22px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#123f4d'}}>{stay.title}</span>
                </div>
            </header>

            <main className="container" style={{maxWidth: '1050px', margin: '24px auto', padding: '0 16px'}}>

                <section style={{position: 'relative', marginBottom: '24px'}}>
                    {mainImages.length > 0 ? (
