File: ./src/main/java/org/example/hotelbooking/domain/reservation/service/ReservationService.java
========================================
package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.reservation.service;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.member.entity.Member;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.member.repository.MemberRepository;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.reservation.entity.Reservation;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.reservation.repository.ReservationRepository;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.room.entity.Room;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.room.entity.RoomInventory;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.room.repository.RoomInventoryRepository;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.room.repository.RoomRepository;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class ReservationService {

    private final ReservationRepository reservationRepository;
    private final RoomInventoryRepository roomInventoryRepository;
    private final MemberRepository memberRepository;
    private final RoomRepository roomRepository;

    @Transactional
    public void createReservation(Long memberId, Long roomId, LocalDate checkIn, LocalDate checkOut, Integer totalPrice) {
        // 1. 체크인 ~ 체크아웃 전날(-1일)까지의 재고 조회
        List<RoomInventory> inventories = roomInventoryRepository
                .findInventoriesForReservation(roomId, checkIn, checkOut.minusDays(1));

        // 2. 요청한 숙박 일수와 DB에 세팅된 재고 일수가 일치하는지 검증
        long days = java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(checkIn, checkOut);
        if (inventories.size() != days) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("예약할 수 없는 날짜가 포함되어 있습니다.");
        }

        // 3. 재고 차감 (Dirty Checking으로 자동 UPDATE 쿼리 발생)
        for (RoomInventory inventory : inventories) {
            inventory.decreaseCount(1);
        }

        // 4. 예약 정보 저장
        Member member = memberRepository.findById(memberId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("존재하지 않는 회원입니다."));
        Room room = roomRepository.findById(roomId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("존재하지 않는 객실입니다."));

        Reservation reservation = Reservation.builder()
                .member(member)
                .room(room)
                .checkInDate(checkIn)
                .checkOutDate(checkOut)
                .totalPrice(totalPrice)
                .status("COMPLETED")
                .build();

        reservationRepository.save(reservation);
    }
    // 기존 createReservation 아래에 추가
    @Transactional
    public void cancelReservation(Long reservationId, Long memberId) {
        // 1. 예약 정보 조회
        Reservation reservation = reservationRepository.findById(reservationId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("존재하지 않는 예약입니다."));

        // 2. 본인의 예약인지 검증
        if (!reservation.getMember().getMemberId().equals(memberId)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("본인의 예약만 취소할 수 있습니다.");
        }

        // 3. 예약 상태 취소로 변경 (Dirty Checking)
        reservation.cancel();

        // 4. 재고 복구
        List<RoomInventory> inventories = roomInventoryRepository.findInventoriesForReservation(
                reservation.getRoom().getRoomId(),
                reservation.getCheckInDate(),
                reservation.getCheckOutDate().minusDays(1)
        );

        for (RoomInventory inventory : inventories) {
            inventory.increaseCount(1);
        }
    }
}

package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.review.entity;

import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.Accommodation;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.member.entity.Member;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.reservation.entity.Reservation;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Entity
@Getter
@NoArgsConstructor(access = AccessLevel.PROTECTED)
@Table(name = "REVIEW")
public class Review {

    @Id @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    @Column(name = "REVIEW_ID")
    private Long reviewId;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "MEMBER_ID", nullable = false)
    private Member member;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "CONTENT_ID", nullable = false)
    private Accommodation accommodation;

    @OneToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "RESERVATION_ID", nullable = false, unique = true)
    private Reservation reservation;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private Integer rating;

    @Lob
    @Column(nullable = false)
    private String content;

    @Column(name = "CREATED_AT", updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @PrePersist
    protected void onCreate() {
        this.createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto;

import lombok.Builder;
import lombok.Getter;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.Accommodation;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.room.entity.Room;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.room.entity.RoomImage;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@Getter
@Builder
public class StayDetailResponseDto {
    private String contentId;
    private String title;
    private String lodgingType;
    private String tel;
    private String zipcode;
    private String addr;
    private String checkinTime;
    private String checkoutTime;
    private String roomCount;
    private String roomType;
    private String parking;
    private String subFacility;
    private String overview;
    private String firstImage;
    private String firstImage2;
    private String mapX;
    private String mapY;
    private String hostName;
    private String businessNo;
    private List<RoomDto> rooms;

    @Getter
    @Builder
    public static class RoomDto {
        private Long roomId;
        private String roomTitle;
        private String roomSize;
        private String roomBaseCount;
        private String roomMaxCount;
        private Integer pricePerNight;
        private List<String> imageUrls;
    }

    public static StayDetailResponseDto of(Accommodation a, List<Room> rooms, Map<Long, List<RoomImage>> roomImagesMap) {
        return StayDetailResponseDto.builder()
                .contentId(a.getContentId())
                .title(a.getTitle())
                .lodgingType(a.getLodgingType())
                .tel(a.getTel())
                .zipcode(a.getZipcode())
                .addr(a.getAddr())
                .checkinTime(a.getCheckinTime())
                .checkoutTime(a.getCheckoutTime())
                .roomCount(a.getRoomCount())
                .roomType(a.getRoomType())
                .parking(a.getParking())
                .subFacility(a.getSubFacility())
                .overview(a.getOverview())
                .firstImage(a.getFirstImage())
                .firstImage2(a.getFirstImage2())
                .mapX(a.getMapX())
                .mapY(a.getMapY())
                .hostName(a.getHost() != null ? a.getHost().getName() : "제주야호 파트너")
                // 💡 2. 호스트가 존재하면 MEMBER 테이블의 businessNo 추출, 없으면 기본값 반환
                .businessNo(a.getHost() != null && a.getHost().getBusinessNo() != null
                        ? a.getHost().getBusinessNo()
                        : "등록된 사업자번호 없음")
                .rooms(rooms.stream().map(r -> {
                    List<RoomImage> rImages = roomImagesMap.getOrDefault(r.getRoomId(), List.of());

                    List<String> urls = rImages.stream()
                            .map(RoomImage::getImageUrl)
                            .toList();

                    return RoomDto.builder()
                            .roomId(r.getRoomId())
                            .roomTitle(r.getRoomTitle())
                            .roomSize(r.getRoomSize())
                            .roomBaseCount(r.getRoomBaseCount())
                            .roomMaxCount(r.getRoomMaxCount())
                            .pricePerNight(r.getPricePerNight())
                            .imageUrls(urls)
                            .build();
                }).toList())
                .build();
    }
}

package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto;

import lombok.*;
import java.math.BigDecimal;

@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
public class StayResponseDto {
    private String contentId;
    private Long memberId;
    private String title;
    private String lodgingType;
    private String tel;
    private String zipcode;
    private String addr;
    private String checkinTime;
    private String checkoutTime;
    private String roomCount;
    private String roomType;
    private String parking;
    private String subFacility;
    private String overview;
    private String firstImage;
    private String firstImage2;
    private String mapX;
    private String mapY;
    private BigDecimal basePrice; // 객실 최저가

    // 기존 BigDecimal 생성자 유지
    public StayResponseDto(String contentId, Long memberId, String title, String lodgingType,
                           String tel, String zipcode, String addr, String checkinTime,
                           String checkoutTime, String roomCount, String roomType,
                           String parking, String subFacility, String overview,
                           String firstImage, String firstImage2, String mapX, String mapY,
                           BigDecimal basePrice) {
        this.contentId = contentId;
        this.memberId = memberId;
        this.title = title;
        this.lodgingType = lodgingType;
        this.tel = tel;
        this.zipcode = zipcode;
        this.addr = addr;
        this.checkinTime = checkinTime;
        this.checkoutTime = checkoutTime;
        this.roomCount = roomCount;
        this.roomType = roomType;
        this.parking = parking;
        this.subFacility = subFacility;
        this.overview = overview;
        this.firstImage = firstImage;
        this.firstImage2 = firstImage2;
        this.mapX = mapX;
        this.mapY = mapY;
        this.basePrice = (basePrice != null) ? basePrice : BigDecimal.ZERO;
    }

    // 💡 JPQL 매핑용 Integer basePrice 생성자 추가
    public StayResponseDto(String contentId, Long memberId, String title, String lodgingType,
                           String tel, String zipcode, String addr, String checkinTime,
                           String checkoutTime, String roomCount, String roomType,
                           String parking, String subFacility, String overview,
                           String firstImage, String firstImage2, String mapX, String mapY,
                           Integer basePrice) {
        this(contentId, memberId, title, lodgingType, tel, zipcode, addr, checkinTime,
                checkoutTime, roomCount, roomType, parking, subFacility, overview,
                firstImage, firstImage2, mapX, mapY,
                basePrice != null ? BigDecimal.valueOf(basePrice) : BigDecimal.ZERO);
    }
}

package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.repository;

import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.Accommodation;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.StayResponseDto;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.AccommodationStatus;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

import java.util.List;

public interface AccommodationRepository extends JpaRepository<Accommodation, String>, AccommodationRepositoryCustom {

    @Query("SELECT new org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.StayResponseDto(" +
            "a.contentId, a.host.memberId, a.title, a.lodgingType, a.tel, a.zipcode, a.addr, " +
            "a.checkinTime, a.checkoutTime, a.roomCount, a.roomType, a.parking, a.subFacility, " +
            "a.overview, a.firstImage, a.firstImage2, a.mapX, a.mapY, " +
            "COALESCE((SELECT MIN(r.pricePerNight) FROM Room r WHERE r.accommodation = a), 0)) " +
            "FROM Accommodation a " +
            "WHERE a.approvalStatus = org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.AccommodationStatus.APPROVED " +
            "  AND (:region IS NULL OR :region = '' OR :region = '전체' OR a.addr LIKE %:region%) " +
            "  AND (:type IS NULL OR :type = '' OR :type = '전체' OR a.lodgingType LIKE %:type%) " +
            "  AND (:query IS NULL OR :query = '' OR a.title LIKE %:query% OR a.addr LIKE %:query%) " +
            "ORDER BY a.contentId DESC")
    List<StayResponseDto> searchStays(
            @Param("region") String region,
            @Param("type") String type,
            @Param("query") String query
    );
    // 숙소 생성시 시퀀스 생성
    @Query(value = "SELECT SEQ_HOST_ACCOM.NEXTVAL FROM DUAL", nativeQuery = true)
    Long getNextHostAccomSeq();

    // 호스트가 등록한 숙소 찾아오는 jpa쿼리
    List<Accommodation> findByHost_Email(String email);

    // 숙소 관리자의 숙소 보유 여부 확인
    boolean existsByHost_MemberIdAndApprovalStatus(Long memberId, org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.AccommodationStatus approvalStatus);

    // 특정 호스트의 반려된 숙소 목록 조회 (반려 알림용)
    List<Accommodation> findByHost_MemberIdAndApprovalStatus(Long memberId, AccommodationStatus approvalStatus);

    // 총괄 관리자용: 승인 대기(PENDING) 상태 숙소 목록 조회 (심사 알림용)
    List<Accommodation> findByApprovalStatusOrderByCreatedAtDesc(AccommodationStatus approvalStatus);

    // pending 상태인 숙소 목록 가져오는 메서드
    List<Accommodation> findByApprovalStatus(AccommodationStatus approvalStatus);
}

package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.repository;

import com.querydsl.jpa.impl.JPAQueryFactory;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.Accommodation;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.AccommodationStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

import java.util.List;

// QAccommodation 임포트 (빨간 줄 뜨면 Build -> Build Project 한 번 눌러주세요)
import static org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.QAccommodation.accommodation;

@Repository
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AccommodationRepositoryImpl implements AccommodationRepositoryCustom {

    private final JPAQueryFactory queryFactory;


    @Override
    public List<Accommodation> findAccommodationsInMap(String minX, String maxX, String minY, String maxY) {
        return queryFactory
                .selectFrom(accommodation)
                .where(
                        accommodation.approvalStatus.eq(AccommodationStatus.APPROVED),
                        accommodation.mapX.between(minX, maxX),
                        accommodation.mapY.between(minY, maxY)
                )
                .fetch();


    }



}

public interface AccommodationRepositoryCustom {
    // 화면의 남서쪽(min)과 북동쪽(max) 좌표를 받아 그 안의 '승인된' 숙소만 검색

package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity;
// 숙소 상태 enum 추가
public enum AccommodationStatus {
    PENDING,  // 승인 대기 (진행중)
    APPROVED, // 승인 완료 (홈페이지 노출)
    REJECTED  // 반려
}
========================================
File: ./src/main/java/org/example/hotelbooking/domain/accommodation/entity/Accommodation.java
========================================
package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity;

import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.member.entity.Member;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Entity
@Getter
@NoArgsConstructor(access = AccessLevel.PROTECTED)
@Table(name = "ACCOMMODATION")
public class Accommodation {

    @Id
    @Column(name = "CONTENT_ID", length = 20)
    private String contentId;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 200)
    private String title;

    @Column(name = "LODGING_TYPE", length = 50)
    private String lodgingType;

    @Column(length = 100)
    private String tel;

    @Column(length = 10)
    private String zipcode;

    @Column(length = 300)
    private String addr;

    @Column(name = "CHECKIN_TIME", length = 100)
    private String checkinTime;

    @Column(name = "CHECKOUT_TIME", length = 100)
    private String checkoutTime;

    @Column(name = "ROOM_COUNT", length = 50)
    private String roomCount;

    @Column(name = "ROOM_TYPE", length = 200)
    private String roomType;

    @Column(length = 300)
    private String parking;

    @Column(name = "SUB_FACILITY", length = 1000)
    private String subFacility;

    @Lob
    private String overview;

    @Column(name = "FIRST_IMAGE", length = 1000)
    private String firstImage;

    @Column(name = "FIRST_IMAGE2", length = 1000)
    private String firstImage2;

    @Column(name = "MAP_X", length = 50)
    private String mapX;

    @Column(name = "MAP_Y", length = 50)
    private String mapY;

    // 기존 APPROVAL_STATUS 컬럼을 Enum(PENDING, APPROVED, REJECTED)으로 매핑
    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "APPROVAL_STATUS", length = 20, nullable = false)
    private AccommodationStatus approvalStatus;

    @Column(name = "REJECT_REASON", length = 255)
    private String rejectReason;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "MEMBER_ID")
    private Member host;

    @Column(name = "CREATED_AT", updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @PrePersist
    protected void onCreate() {
        this.createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (this.approvalStatus == null) {
            this.approvalStatus = AccommodationStatus.PENDING;
        }
    }

    // 관리자 승인/반려 편의 메서드
    public void approve() {
        this.approvalStatus = AccommodationStatus.APPROVED;
        this.rejectReason = null;
    }

    public void reject(String reason) {
        this.approvalStatus = AccommodationStatus.REJECTED;
        this.rejectReason = reason;
    }

    @Builder
    public Accommodation(String contentId, String title, String lodgingType, String tel, String zipcode,
                         String addr, String checkinTime, String checkoutTime, String roomCount,
                         String roomType, String parking, String subFacility, String overview,
                         String firstImage, String firstImage2, String mapX, String mapY,
                         Member host, AccommodationStatus approvalStatus, String rejectReason) {
        this.contentId = contentId;
        this.title = title;
        this.lodgingType = lodgingType;
        this.tel = tel;
        this.zipcode = zipcode;
        this.addr = addr;
        this.checkinTime = checkinTime;
        this.checkoutTime = checkoutTime;
        this.roomCount = roomCount;
        this.roomType = roomType;
        this.parking = parking;
        this.subFacility = subFacility;
        this.overview = overview;
        this.firstImage = firstImage;
        this.firstImage2 = firstImage2;
        this.mapX = mapX;
        this.mapY = mapY;
        this.host = host;
        this.approvalStatus = (approvalStatus != null) ? approvalStatus : AccommodationStatus.PENDING;
        this.rejectReason = rejectReason;
    }
}

package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.controller;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.AccommodationDto;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.AccommodationResponse;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.service.AccommodationService;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
import org.springframework.security.core.annotation.AuthenticationPrincipal;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/accommodations")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AccommodationController {

    private final AccommodationService accommodationService;

    // 기존에 만드신 지도 기반 숙소 조회 API (유지)
    @GetMapping
    public ResponseEntity<List<AccommodationResponse>> getMapAccommodations(
            @RequestParam String minX,
            @RequestParam String maxX,
            @RequestParam String minY,
            @RequestParam String maxY) {

        List<AccommodationResponse> response = accommodationService.getAccommodationsInMap(minX, maxX, minY, maxY);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }

    // 💡 새롭게 추가하는 숙소 등록 API
    @PreAuthorize("hasRole('HOST')")
    @PostMapping("/register")
    public ResponseEntity<String> register(
            @RequestBody AccommodationDto.RegisterRequest request,
            @AuthenticationPrincipal String email // JWT 필터에서 넣어둔 유저 이메일
    ) {
        String contentId = accommodationService.register(email, request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok("숙소 등록 완료! 숙소 ID: " + contentId);
    }
    @PreAuthorize("hasRole('HOST')")
    @GetMapping("/my")
    public ResponseEntity<List<AccommodationResponse>> getMyAccommodations(
            // 혹시 CustomUserDetails 같은 객체로 꺼내야 한다면 여기서 에러가 나거나 null일 수 있습니다.
            @AuthenticationPrincipal String email
    ) {
        // 💡 콘솔창에 이메일이 정상적으로 찍히는지 확인하는 코드!
        System.out.println("토큰에서 꺼낸 이메일: " + email);

        List<AccommodationResponse> myAccommodations = accommodationService.getMyAccommodations(email);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(myAccommodations);
    }

    // 💡 승인된 숙소 보유 여부 확인 API
    @GetMapping("/has-approved")
    public ResponseEntity<Boolean> checkApprovedAccommodation(@AuthenticationPrincipal String email) {
        if (email == null) {
            return ResponseEntity.ok(false);
        }
        boolean hasApproved = accommodationService.hasApprovedAccommodation(email);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(hasApproved);
    }
}

package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.controller;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.AccommodationResponse;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.AdminRejectRequest;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.service.AccommodationService;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;

@RestController
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/admin/accommodations")
@PreAuthorize("hasRole('ADMIN')") // 💡 관리자 권한 필수
public class AdminAccommodationController {

    private final AccommodationService accommodationService;

    // 1. 대기 중인 숙소 목록 조회
    @GetMapping("/pending")
    public ResponseEntity<List<AccommodationResponse>> getPendingAccommodations() {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(accommodationService.getPendingAccommodations());
    }

    // 2. 숙소 승인
    @PatchMapping("/{contentId}/approve")
    public ResponseEntity<String> approveAccommodation(@PathVariable String contentId) {
        accommodationService.approveAccommodation(contentId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok("숙소 ID " + contentId + "의 승인이 완료되었습니다.");
    }

    // 3. 숙소 반려
    @PatchMapping("/{contentId}/reject")
    public ResponseEntity<String> rejectAccommodation(
            @PathVariable String contentId,
            @RequestBody AdminRejectRequest request
    ) {
        accommodationService.rejectAccommodation(contentId, request.getRejectReason());
        return ResponseEntity.ok("숙소 ID " + contentId + "가 반려 처리되었습니다.");
    }
}

package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.controller;


import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.StayDetailResponseDto;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.StayResponseDto;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.service.StayService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;

@CrossOrigin(origins = "http://localhost:5173")
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class StayController {

    private final StayService stayService;

    @GetMapping("/stays")
    public ResponseEntity<List<StayResponseDto>> getStays(
            @RequestParam(value = "region", required = false) String region,
            @RequestParam(value = "type", required = false) String type,
            @RequestParam(value = "query", required = false) String query
    ) {
        List<StayResponseDto> list = stayService.getStays(region, type, query);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(list);
    }

    @GetMapping("/stays/{contentId}")
    public ResponseEntity<StayDetailResponseDto> getStayDetail(@PathVariable String contentId) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(stayService.getStayDetail(contentId));
    }
}

File: ./src/main/java/org/example/hotelbooking/domain/accommodation/service/StayService.java

package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.service;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.StayDetailResponseDto;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.StayResponseDto;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.Accommodation;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.repository.AccommodationRepository;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.room.entity.Room;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.room.entity.RoomImage;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.room.repository.RoomImageRepository;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.room.repository.RoomRepository;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Transactional(readOnly = true)
public class StayService {

    private final AccommodationRepository accommodationRepository;
    private final RoomRepository roomRepository;
    private final RoomImageRepository roomImageRepository;

    public List<StayResponseDto> getStays(String region, String type, String query) {
        return accommodationRepository.searchStays(region, type, query);
    }

    public StayDetailResponseDto getStayDetail(String contentId) {
        Accommodation accommodation = accommodationRepository.findById(contentId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("숙소를 찾을 수 없습니다. contentId=" + contentId));

        List<Room> rooms = roomRepository.findByAccommodation_ContentId(contentId);

        Map<Long, List<RoomImage>> roomImagesMap = new HashMap<>();
        for (Room room : rooms) {
            List<RoomImage> images = roomImageRepository.findByRoom_RoomIdOrderByDisplayOrderAsc(room.getRoomId());
            for (RoomImage img : images) {
            }
            roomImagesMap.put(room.getRoomId(), images);
        }

        return StayDetailResponseDto.of(accommodation, rooms, roomImagesMap);
    }
}

File: ./src/main/java/org/example/hotelbooking/domain/accommodation/service/AccommodationService.java
========================================
package org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.service;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.AccommodationDto;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.dto.AccommodationResponse;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.Accommodation;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.entity.AccommodationStatus;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.accommodation.repository.AccommodationRepository;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.member.entity.Member;
import org.example.hotelbooking.domain.member.repository.MemberRepository;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Transactional(readOnly = true) // 전체는 읽기 전용으로 성능 최적화
public class AccommodationService {

    private final AccommodationRepository accommodationRepository;
    private final MemberRepository memberRepository; // 💡 호스트 정보를 찾기 위해 추가!

    // 기존에 만드신 지도 조회 기능 (유지)
    public List<AccommodationResponse> getAccommodationsInMap(String minX, String maxX, String minY, String maxY) {
        return accommodationRepository.findAccommodationsInMap(minX, maxX, minY, maxY)
                .stream()
                .map(AccommodationResponse::from)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    // 💡 새롭게 추가하는 숙소 등록 기능
    @Transactional // 쓰기(Save) 작업이므로 이 메서드만 readOnly 옵션을 해제합니다.
    public String register(String email, AccommodationDto.RegisterRequest request) {

        Member host = memberRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("사용자 정보를 찾을 수 없습니다."));

        String contentId = request.getContentId();
        if (contentId == null || contentId.isBlank()) {
            // 💡 오라클 시퀀스에서 다음 번호(1, 2, 3...)를 가져옵니다.
            Long seqNumber = accommodationRepository.getNextHostAccomSeq();
            // 1부터 들어가기
            contentId = String.valueOf(seqNumber);
        }

        Accommodation accommodation = Accommodation.builder()
                .contentId(contentId)
                .title(request.getTitle())
                .lodgingType(request.getLodgingType())
                .tel(request.getTel())
                .zipcode(request.getZipcode())
                .addr(request.getAddr())
                .checkinTime(request.getCheckinTime())
                .checkoutTime(request.getCheckoutTime())
                .roomCount(request.getRoomCount())
                .roomType(request.getRoomType())
                .parking(request.getParking())
                .subFacility(request.getSubFacility())
                .overview(request.getOverview())
                .host(host) // JWT 토큰에서 꺼낸 유저를 숙소 주인으로 등록
                .approvalStatus(AccommodationStatus.PENDING)
                .build();

        accommodationRepository.save(accommodation);

        return accommodation.getContentId();
    }

    // 💡호스트 이메일로 내 숙소 목록 가져오기
    public List<AccommodationResponse> getMyAccommodations(String email) {
        return accommodationRepository.findByHost_Email(email)
                .stream()
                .map(AccommodationResponse::from)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    // 숙소 관리자 숙소 보유 여부 확인(호스트 이메일로 회원을 찾아 승인된(APPROVED) 숙소가 있는지 체크)
    public boolean hasApprovedAccommodation(String email) {
        Member host = memberRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("사용자 정보를 찾을 수 없습니다."));

        // 💡 문자열 "APPROVED" 대신 Enum 상수 AccommodationStatus.APPROVED 전달
        return accommodationRepository.existsByHost_MemberIdAndApprovalStatus(host.getMemberId(), AccommodationStatus.APPROVED);
    }

    // 💡 [총괄관리자용] 승인 대기(PENDING) 숙소 목록 조회
    public List<AccommodationResponse> getPendingAccommodations() {
        return accommodationRepository.findByApprovalStatus(AccommodationStatus.PENDING)
                .stream()
                .map(AccommodationResponse::from)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    // 💡 [총괄관리자용] 숙소 승인 처리
    @Transactional
    public void approveAccommodation(String contentId) {
        Accommodation accommodation = accommodationRepository.findById(contentId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("존재하지 않는 숙소입니다. id=" + contentId));

        accommodation.approve(); // 엔티티 편의 메서드 호출 -> APPROVED로 변경
    }

    // 💡 [총괄관리자용] 숙소 반려 처리
    @Transactional
    public void rejectAccommodation(String contentId, String rejectReason) {
        if (rejectReason == null || rejectReason.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("반려 사유를 입력해야 합니다.");
        }

        Accommodation accommodation = accommodationRepository.findById(contentId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("존재하지 않는 숙소입니다. id=" + contentId));

        accommodation.reject(rejectReason); // 엔티티 편의 메서드 호출 -> REJECTED 및 사유 저장
    }
}
========================================
File: ./src/main/java/org/example/hotelbooking/global/config/QueryDslConfig.java
========================================
package org.example.hotelbooking.global.config;

import com.querydsl.jpa.impl.JPAQueryFactory;
import jakarta.persistence.EntityManager;
import jakarta.persistence.PersistenceContext;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

@Configuration
public class QueryDslConfig {

    @PersistenceContext
    private EntityManager entityManager;

    // JPAQueryFactory가 필요하면 이 메서드를 실행해서 가져다 쓰렴!
    @Bean
    public JPAQueryFactory jpaQueryFactory() {
        return new JPAQueryFactory(entityManager);
    }
}

File: ./src/main/java/org/example/hotelbooking/global/security/SecurityConfig.java
========================================
package org.example.hotelbooking.global.security;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.security.config.Customizer;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.configuration.EnableWebSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.config.http.SessionCreationPolicy;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.bcrypt.BCryptPasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.security.web.SecurityFilterChain;
import org.springframework.security.web.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter;
import org.springframework.web.cors.CorsConfiguration;
import org.springframework.web.cors.CorsConfigurationSource;
import org.springframework.web.cors.UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource;

import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

@Configuration
@EnableWebSecurity
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class SecurityConfig {

    private final JwtTokenProvider jwtTokenProvider;

    @Bean
    public SecurityFilterChain filterChain(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {
        http
                // React(Vite)와 통신하기 위한 CORS 설정
                .cors(Customizer.withDefaults())

                // REST API이므로 CSRF 보호 비활성화
                .csrf(csrf -> csrf.disable())

                // JWT를 사용하므로 서버 세션을 사용하지 않음
                .sessionManagement(session ->
                        session.sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS)
                )

                // API 접근 권한 설정
                .authorizeHttpRequests(auth -> auth

                        // 로그인 / 회원가입
                        .requestMatchers("/api/v1/auth/**").permitAll()

                        // 숙소 조회
                        .requestMatchers("/api/v1/accommodations/**").permitAll()

                        // 기존 psj의 숙소 조회 API
                        .requestMatchers("/api/v1/stays/**").permitAll()

                        // 비밀번호 찾기 위한 허용 설정
                        .requestMatchers("/api/v1/members/password/**").permitAll()

                        // 그 외 요청은 JWT 인증 필요
                        .anyRequest().authenticated()
                )

                // JWT 인증 필터 등록
                .addFilterBefore(
                        new JwtAuthenticationFilter(jwtTokenProvider),
                        UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter.class
                );

        return http.build();
    }

    // 비밀번호 암호화
    @Bean
    public PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder() {
        return new BCryptPasswordEncoder();
    }

    // React 개발 서버 CORS 설정
    @Bean
    public CorsConfigurationSource corsConfigurationSource() {
        CorsConfiguration configuration = new CorsConfiguration();

        configuration.setAllowedOrigins(
                List.of("http://localhost:5173")
        );

        configuration.setAllowedMethods(
                Arrays.asList(
                        "GET",
                        "POST",
                        "PUT",
                        "DELETE",
                        "OPTIONS",
                        "PATCH"
                )
        );

        configuration.setAllowedHeaders(List.of("*"));
        configuration.setAllowCredentials(true);
        configuration.setMaxAge(3600L);

        UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource source =
                new UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource();

        source.registerCorsConfiguration("/**", configuration);

        return source;
    }
}

File: ./src/main/java/org/example/hotelbooking/global/security/JwtAuthenticationFilter.java
========================================
package org.example.hotelbooking.global.security;

import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.security.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
import org.springframework.security.core.authority.SimpleGrantedAuthority;
import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;
import org.springframework.web.filter.OncePerRequestFilter;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Collections;

// 💡 롬복을 써서 JwtTokenProvider를 자동으로 주입받습니다.
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class JwtAuthenticationFilter extends OncePerRequestFilter {

    private final JwtTokenProvider jwtTokenProvider;

    @Override
    protected void doFilterInternal(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response, FilterChain filterChain)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        // 1. 사용자 요청 헤더에서 JWT 토큰을 쓱 뽑아옵니다. (아래 만들어둔 resolveToken 메서드 사용)
        String token = resolveToken(request);

        // 2. 토큰이 비어있지 않고, 진품(유효기간 안 지남 등)인지 검사합니다.
        if (token != null && jwtTokenProvider.validateToken(token)) {

            // 3. 진품이라면 토큰 안에 적혀있는 사용자 이메일(Subject)과 역할(Role)을 꺼냅니다.
            String email = jwtTokenProvider.getUserEmail(token);
            String role = jwtTokenProvider.getUserRole(token);

            // 4. 꺼낸 정보를 바탕으로 "이 사람은 인증된 사용자입니다" 라는 확인증(Authentication 객체)을 만듭니다.
            Authentication authentication = new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(
                    email,
                    null,
                    Collections.singletonList(new SimpleGrantedAuthority(role))
            );

            // 5. 스프링 시큐리티의 핵심 저장소(SecurityContext)에 이 확인증을 예쁘게 보관해 둡니다.
            //    (이렇게 해두면 나중에 컨트롤러에서 @AuthenticationPrincipal 로 이 사람 정보를 꺼내 쓸 수 있습니다!)
            SecurityContextHolder.getContext().setAuthentication(authentication);
        }

        // 6. 검사가 끝났으니 다음 필터나 컨트롤러로 무사히 넘겨줍니다.
        filterChain.doFilter(request, response);
    }

    // 💡 프론트엔드가 보낸 요청(Header) 주머니에서 "Bearer xxxx.yyyy.zzzz" 형태의 토큰만 쏙 빼내는 기능
    private String resolveToken(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String bearerToken = request.getHeader("Authorization");
        // 토큰이 존재하고, "Bearer "로 시작하면 (7글자)
        if (StringUtils.hasText(bearerToken) && bearerToken.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            return bearerToken.substring(7); // "Bearer " 이후의 진짜 토큰 부분만 잘라서 반환
        }
        return null; // 토큰이 없으면 null 반환
    }
}

File: ./src/main/java/org/example/hotelbooking/global/security/JwtTokenProvider.java
========================================
package org.example.hotelbooking.global.security;

import io.jsonwebtoken.*;
import io.jsonwebtoken.io.Decoders;
import io.jsonwebtoken.security.Keys;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.security.Key;
import java.util.Date;

@Slf4j
@Component
public class JwtTokenProvider {

    private final Key key;
    private final long expirationTime;

    // yml 또는 properties 파일의 값을 불러와서 Key 객체로 만듭니다.
    public JwtTokenProvider(@Value("${jwt.secret}") String secretKey,
                            @Value("${jwt.expiration}") long expirationTime) {
        byte[] keyBytes = Decoders.BASE64.decode(secretKey);
        this.key = Keys.hmacShaKeyFor(keyBytes);
        this.expirationTime = expirationTime;
    }

    // 💡 1. 토큰 생성 (로그인 성공 시 프론트엔드로 내려줄 출입증)
    // 프론트엔드에서 로그인 시 실제 닉네임을 띄우기 위해 String name, .claim("name", name) 추가
    public String createToken(String email, String role, String name) {
        Date now = new Date();
        Date validity = new Date(now.getTime() + this.expirationTime);

        return Jwts.builder()
                .setSubject(email) // 토큰 주인을 이메일로 기록
                .claim("role", role) // 권한 정보 기록 (예: ROLE_USER)
                .claim("name", name) // 💡 토큰 claim에 DB name 추가
                .setIssuedAt(now) // 발행 시간
                .setExpiration(validity) // 만료 시간
                .signWith(key, SignatureAlgorithm.HS256) // 암호화 알고리즘
                .compact();
    }

    // 💡 2. 토큰 유효성 검증 (API 요청이 올 때마다 진짜 출입증인지 확인)
    public boolean validateToken(String token) {
        try {
            Jwts.parserBuilder().setSigningKey(key).build().parseClaimsJws(token);
            return true;
        } catch (io.jsonwebtoken.security.SecurityException | MalformedJwtException e) {
            log.info("잘못된 JWT 서명입니다.");
        } catch (ExpiredJwtException e) {
            log.info("만료된 JWT 토큰입니다.");
        } catch (UnsupportedJwtException e) {
            log.info("지원되지 않는 JWT 토큰입니다.");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            log.info("JWT 토큰이 잘못되었습니다.");
        }
        return false;

    }
    // 💡 3. 토큰을 분해해서 주인의 이메일(Subject)을 꺼내는 기능
    public String getUserEmail(String token) {
        return Jwts.parserBuilder().setSigningKey(key).build()
                .parseClaimsJws(token)
                .getBody()
                .getSubject();
    }

    // 💡 4. 토큰을 분해해서 주인의 권한(Role)을 꺼내는 기능
    public String getUserRole(String token) {
        return Jwts.parserBuilder().setSigningKey(key).build()
                .parseClaimsJws(token)
                .getBody()
                .get("role", String.class);
    }
}

import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';

    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    stays: Stay[];

    // 1. 모달 활성화 시 배경 스크롤 방지

        if (isOpen) {

            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';

            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';

        <div
            style={{

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';
import { money, getLodgingTypeName } from '../data/stays';

declare const window: typeof globalThis & {
    kakao: any;
};

interface KakaoStayMapProps {
    stays: Stay[];
    level?: number;
    height?: string;
    mode?: 'search' | 'detail'; // 💡 모드 구분 (기본값: 'search')
}

const KAKAO_MAP_KEY = '1eb6f2a2617e0c3b3bb7eb7b90331bae';

export function KakaoStayMap({ stays, level, height = '520px', mode = 'search' }: KakaoStayMapProps) {
    const mapContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [isLoaded, setIsLoaded] = useState(false);

    // 1. 카카오 지도 SDK 동적 주입
    useEffect(() => {
        const existingScript = document.getElementById('kakao-map-sdk');
        if (existingScript) {
            if (window.kakao && window.kakao.maps) {
                setIsLoaded(true);
            } else {
                existingScript.addEventListener('load', () => setIsLoaded(true));
            }
            return;
        }

        const script = document.createElement('script');
        script.id = 'kakao-map-sdk';
        script.src = `//dapi.kakao.com/v2/maps/sdk.js?appkey=${KAKAO_MAP_KEY}&libraries=clusterer&autoload=false`;
        script.async = true;

        script.onload = () => {
            setIsLoaded(true);
        };

        document.head.appendChild(script);
    }, []);

    // 2. 지도 초기화 및 마커 렌더링
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isLoaded || !window.kakao || !window.kakao.maps) return;

        window.kakao.maps.load(() => {
            if (!mapContainerRef.current) return;

            const validStays = stays.filter(
                (s) => s.mapX && s.mapY && !isNaN(Number(s.mapX)) && !isNaN(Number(s.mapY))
            );

            // 💡 상세페이지 모드인지 여부 (mode가 'detail'일 때만 작동)
            const isDetailPage = mode === 'detail';
            const isSingleStay = validStays.length === 1;

            // 중심 좌표: 상세페이지이거나 검색 결과가 딱 1곳이면 해당 숙소 좌표, 그 외는 제주도 전역
            const centerPosition = (isDetailPage || isSingleStay) && validStays.length > 0
                ? new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(
                    parseFloat(validStays[0].mapY!),
                    parseFloat(validStays[0].mapX!)
                )
                : new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(33.3617, 126.5292);

            // 줌 레벨: level prop 우선 -> 상세페이지면 4(근접), 검색 모달이면 숙소 1개여도 적당히 주변이 보이는 6~7 또는 지정된 레벨
            const zoomLevel = level ?? (isDetailPage ? 4 : isSingleStay ? 6 : 9);

            const options = {
                center: centerPosition,
                level: zoomLevel,
            };
            const map = new window.kakao.maps.Map(mapContainerRef.current, options);

            // =========================================================
            // 💡 [케이스 A] 객실 상세페이지 전용 뷰 (mode === 'detail')
            // : 숙박종류 + 숙소명 + 상세 주소 + 주소 복사 버튼 (가격/상세보기 제외)
            // =========================================================
            if (isDetailPage && validStays.length > 0) {
                const stay = validStays[0];
                const marker = new window.kakao.maps.Marker({
                    position: centerPosition,
                    map: map,
                });

                const detailCard = document.createElement('div');
                detailCard.style.cssText = `
                    background: #ffffff;
                    border-radius: 12px;
                    box-shadow: 0 4px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16);
                    border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                    padding: 12px 14px;
                    min-width: 220px;
                    max-width: 280px;
                    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
                    position: relative;
                `;

                // 💡 상단 X(닫기) 버튼 및 주소 복사 버튼 추가
                detailCard.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                        <span style="font-size: 11px; color: #0f766e; font-weight: 700;">
                            ${getLodgingTypeName(stay.lodgingType)}
                        </span>
                        <button id="btn-close-detail-overlay" style="
                            background: none;
                            border: none;
                            font-size: 14px;
                            color: #94a3b8;
                            cursor: pointer;
                            padding: 0 0 0 8px;
                            line-height: 1;
                        ">✕</button>
                    </div>
                    <strong style="display: block; font-size: 14px; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px; line-height: 1.3;">
                        ${stay.title}
                    </strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.4; margin-bottom: 8px; word-break: keep-all;">
                        ${stay.addr || '등록된 주소 정보 없음'}
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                        <button id="btn-copy-stay-addr" style="
                            flex: 1;
                            padding: 6px 0;
                            background-color: #f1f5f9;
                            color: #123f4d;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 11px;
                            font-weight: 600;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            주소 복사
                        </button>
                        <button id="btn-close-bottom" style="
                            padding: 6px 12px;
                            background-color: #ffffff;
                            color: #64748b;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 11px;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            닫기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const overlay = new window.kakao.maps.CustomOverlay({
                    content: detailCard,
                    position: centerPosition,
                    yAnchor: 1.3,
                });

                // 처음에는 열어둠
                overlay.setMap(map);

                // 마커 클릭 시 다시 열기
                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(marker, 'click', () => {
                    overlay.setMap(map);
                });

                // 주소 복사 및 닫기 버튼 이벤트 연결
                setTimeout(() => {
                    const copyBtn = document.getElementById('btn-copy-stay-addr');
                    const closeXBtn = document.getElementById('btn-close-detail-overlay');
                    const closeBottomBtn = document.getElementById('btn-close-bottom');

                    if (copyBtn && stay.addr) {
                        copyBtn.onclick = () => {
                            navigator.clipboard.writeText(stay.addr as string);
                            alert('주소가 클립보드에 복사되었습니다.');
                        };
                    }
                    if (closeXBtn) {
                        closeXBtn.onclick = () => overlay.setMap(null);
                    }
                    if (closeBottomBtn) {
                        closeBottomBtn.onclick = () => overlay.setMap(null);
                    }
                }, 0);

                return;
            }

            // =========================================================
            // 💡 [케이스 B] 검색/홈페이지 지도 모드 (mode === 'search')
            // : 숙소가 1개든 여러 개든 숙박종류 + 이름 + 주소 + 가격 + 상세보기 버튼 전체 노출!
            // =========================================================
            const clusterer = new window.kakao.maps.MarkerClusterer({
                map: map,
                averageCenter: true,
                minLevel: 6,
                disableClickZoom: false,
            });

            let currentOverlay: any = null;

            const markers = validStays.map((stay) => {
                const lat = parseFloat(stay.mapY!);
                const lng = parseFloat(stay.mapX!);
                const position = new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(lat, lng);

                const marker = new window.kakao.maps.Marker({
                    position: position,
                });

                // 카드형 오버레이 UI 엘리먼트 생성
                const content = document.createElement('div');
                content.style.cssText = `
                    background: #ffffff;
                    border-radius: 14px;
                    box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.18);
                    border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                    padding: 14px 16px;
                    width: 230px;
                    position: relative;
                    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
                `;

                content.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #0f766e; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                        ${getLodgingTypeName(stay.lodgingType)}
                    </div>
                    <strong style="display: block; font-size: 14px; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
                        ${stay.title}
                    </strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.3; margin-bottom: 8px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
                        ${stay.addr || ''}
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; color: #123f4d; margin-bottom: 10px;">
                        ${money(stay.basePrice)} <span style="font-size: 11px; font-weight: 400; color: #94a3b8;">/ 1박</span>
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                        <button id="btn-detail-${stay.contentId}" style="
                            flex: 1;
                            padding: 7px 0;
                            background-color: #123f4d;
                            color: #ffffff;
                            border: none;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 12px;
                            font-weight: 600;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            상세보기
                        </button>
                        <button id="btn-close-${stay.contentId}" style="
                            padding: 7px 10px;
                            background-color: #f1f5f9;
                            color: #475569;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                            border-radius: 6px;
                            font-size: 12px;
                            cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            닫기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const overlay = new window.kakao.maps.CustomOverlay({
                    content: content,
                    position: position,
                    yAnchor: 1.25,
                });

                const showOverlay = () => {
                    if (currentOverlay) {
                        currentOverlay.setMap(null);
                    }
                    overlay.setMap(map);
                    currentOverlay = overlay;

                    setTimeout(() => {
                        const detailBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-detail-${stay.contentId}`);
                        const closeBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-close-${stay.contentId}`);

                        if (detailBtn) {
                            detailBtn.onclick = () => navigate(`/stays/${stay.contentId}`);
                        }
                        if (closeBtn) {
                            closeBtn.onclick = () => overlay.setMap(null);
                        }
                    }, 0);
                };

                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(marker, 'click', showOverlay);

                // 💡 만약 검색 결과가 딱 1곳뿐이라면 클릭하지 않아도 카드 오버레이를 자동으로 즉시 열어둠!
                if (isSingleStay) {
                    showOverlay();
                }

                return marker;
            });

            // 검색 결과가 1개일 땐 마커를 지도에 직접 찍고, 2개 이상일 땐 클러스터러 적용
            if (isSingleStay) {
                markers[0].setMap(map);
            } else {
                clusterer.addMarkers(markers);
            }
        });
    }, [isLoaded, stays, level, mode, navigate]);

    return (
        <div
            ref={mapContainerRef}
            style={{
                width: '100%',
                height: height,
                borderRadius: '16px',
                border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                overflow: 'hidden',
                boxShadow: '0 2px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)',
                backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
            }}
        >
            {!isLoaded && (
                <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>
                    지도를 불러오는 중입니다...
                </span>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

import { ArrowRight, MapPin, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { money, getLodgingTypeName } from '../data/stays';
import type { useReservation } from '../hooks/useReservation';

type Reservation = ReturnType<typeof useReservation>;

export function BookingModal({ reservation }: { reservation: Reservation }) {
  const { selected, close } = reservation;
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  if (!selected) return null;

  const bgImage = selected.firstImage || selected.firstImage2 || '/jeju-stays.png';

  const handleGoDetail = () => {
    close();
    navigate(`/stays/${selected.contentId}`);
  };

  return (
      <div className="modal-backdrop" onMouseDown={(e) => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) close(); }}>
        <section className="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="booking-title" style={{ maxWidth: '480px' }}>
          <button className="modal-close" onClick={close} aria-label="닫기">
            <X size={21} />
          </button>
          <div className="modal-image crop-0" style={{ backgroundImage: `url(${bgImage})`, height: '220px' }} />
          <div className="modal-body" style={{ padding: '24px' }}>
            <span className="eyebrow">JEJU STAY</span>
            <h2 id="booking-title" style={{ fontSize: '22px', margin: '4px 0 10px 0' }}>{selected.title}</h2>
            <p className="modal-address" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px', color: '#64748b', fontSize: '13px' }}>
              <MapPin size={16} />
              {selected.addr} · {getLodgingTypeName(selected.lodgingType)}
            </p>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '14px', color: '#475569', lineHeight: 1.6, margin: '14px 0' }}>
              {selected.overview ? `${selected.overview.slice(0, 110)}...` : '아늑하고 편안한 제주 숙소입니다.'}
            </p>
            <div className="modal-price" style={{ margin: '16px 0 20px 0', padding: '12px 16px', background: '#f8fafc', borderRadius: '8px' }}>
              1박 최저가 <strong style={{ color: '#0f766e', fontSize: '18px', marginLeft: '6px' }}>{money(selected.basePrice)}</strong>
            </div>

            <button
                type="button"
                className="reserve-button"
                onClick={handleGoDetail}
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  padding: '14px',
                  backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                  color: '#ffffff',
                  border: 'none',
                  borderRadius: '8px',
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  fontSize: '15px',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  gap: '8px'
                }}
            >
              숙소 상세 및 객실 보러가기 <ArrowRight size={17} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
  );
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/components/NotificationBell.tsx

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Bell, X } from 'lucide-react';

interface NotificationItem {
    id: string;
    title: string;
    message: string;
    type: string;
    createdAt: string;
}

interface NotificationBellProps {
    userRole: string;
    isLoggedIn: boolean;
}

export function NotificationBell({ userRole, isLoggedIn }: NotificationBellProps) {
    const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<NotificationItem[]>([]);
    const [isNotiOpen, setIsNotiOpen] = useState(false);
    const notiRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    // 알림 목록 조회 (ROLE_HOST 및 ROLE_ADMIN 전용)
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isLoggedIn && (userRole === 'ROLE_HOST' || userRole === 'ROLE_ADMIN')) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
            fetch('/api/v1/notifications', {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            })
                .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : []))
                .then((data: NotificationItem[]) => {
                    const dismissed: string[] = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dismissedNotiIds') || '[]');
                    const filtered = data.filter((item) => !dismissed.includes(item.id));
                    setNotifications(filtered);
                })
                .catch(() => setNotifications([]));
        }
    }, [isLoggedIn, userRole]);

    // 알림창 바깥 클릭 시 닫기
    useEffect(() => {
        const handleClickOutsideNoti = (event: MouseEvent) => {
            if (notiRef.current && !notiRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
                setIsNotiOpen(false);
            }
        };
        document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutsideNoti);
        return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutsideNoti);
    }, []);

    // X 버튼 클릭 시 로컬 삭제 처리
    const handleDeleteNoti = (id: string) => {
        const dismissed: string[] = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dismissedNotiIds') || '[]');
        if (!dismissed.includes(id)) {
            dismissed.push(id);
            localStorage.setItem('dismissedNotiIds', JSON.stringify(dismissed));
        }
        setNotifications((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
    };

    // 호스트나 관리자가 아니면 아무것도 렌더링하지 않음
    if (!isLoggedIn || (userRole !== 'ROLE_HOST' && userRole !== 'ROLE_ADMIN')) {
        return null;
    }

    return (
        <div style={{ position: 'relative' }} ref={notiRef}>
            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsNotiOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                style={{
                    position: 'relative',
                    width: '38px',
                    height: '38px',
                    borderRadius: '50%',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    border: '1.5px solid #123f4d',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    color: '#123f4d'
                }}
                aria-label="알림"
            >
                <Bell size={18} />

                {notifications.length > 0 && (
                    <span
                        style={{
                            position: 'absolute',
                            top: '3px',
                            left: '3px',
                            width: '8px',
                            height: '8px',
                            backgroundColor: '#e53e3e',
                            borderRadius: '50%',
                            border: '1.5px solid #ffffff'
                        }}
                    />
                )}
            </button>

            {isNotiOpen && (
                <div
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        top: '115%',
                        right: 0,
                        width: '300px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        boxShadow: '0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        padding: '12px',
                        zIndex: 1100
                    }}
                >
                    <div
                        style={{
                            display: 'flex',
                            justifyContent: 'space-between',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            marginBottom: '8px',
                            paddingBottom: '6px',
                            borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9'
                        }}
                    >
                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#1e293b' }}>알림 내역</strong>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#64748b' }}>{notifications.length}건</span>
                    </div>

                    {notifications.length === 0 ? (
                        <div style={{ padding: '20px 0', textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                            새로운 알림이 없습니다.
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px', maxHeight: '250px', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
                            {notifications.map((item) => (
                                <div
                                    key={item.id}
                                    style={{
                                        display: 'flex',
                                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                        alignItems: 'flex-start',
                                        backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                                        padding: '8px 10px',
                                        borderRadius: '8px',
                                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                        gap: '8px'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <div>
                                        <p
                                            style={{
                                                margin: '0 0 3px 0',
                                                fontSize: '12px',
                                                fontWeight: 600,
                                                color: item.type === 'ACCOM_REJECT' ? '#e53e3e' : '#0f766e'
                                            }}
                                        >
                                            {item.title}
                                        </p>
                                        <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '11px', color: '#475569', wordBreak: 'break-word', lineHeight: '1.4' }}>
                                            {item.message}
                                        </p>
                                    </div>
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={() => handleDeleteNoti(item.id)}
                                        style={{
                                            background: 'none',
                                            border: 'none',
                                            cursor: 'pointer',
                                            padding: '2px',
                                            color: '#94a3b8'
                                        }}
                                        aria-label="알림 지우기"
                                    >
                                        <X size={14} />
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/components/HostApplyBanner.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { requestApplyHost } from '../api/auth';
import axios from 'axios';
import { X, Building } from 'lucide-react';

export function HostApplyBanner() {
    const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [businessNo, setBusinessNo] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (token) {
            try {
                const base64Payload = token.split('.')[1];
                const decodedPayload = JSON.parse(
                    decodeURIComponent(
                        atob(base64Payload)
                            .split('')
                            .map((c) => '%' + ('00' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16)).slice(-2))
                            .join('')
                    )
                );
                // 토큰에서 역할 추출
                setUserRole(decodedPayload.role || null);
            } catch {
                setUserRole(null);
            }
        } else {
            setUserRole(null);
        }
    }, []);

    // 💡 로그인하지 않았거나, 일반 사용자(ROLE_USER)가 아니면 화면에 아예 그리지 않음
    if (userRole !== 'ROLE_USER') {
        return null;
    }

    const handleApply = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!businessNo.trim()) {
            setErrorMessage('사업자등록번호를 입력해주세요.');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);
        setErrorMessage('');

        try {
            const data = await requestApplyHost(businessNo.trim());
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', data.token);
            alert('숙소 관리자 신청이 완료되었습니다! 파트너 권한으로 전환됩니다.');
            setIsModalOpen(false);
            window.location.reload();
        } catch (err: unknown) {
            if (axios.isAxiosError(err) && err.response) {
                setErrorMessage(err.response.data?.message || '신청 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
            } else {
                setErrorMessage('서버와 통신할 수 없습니다.');
            }
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (

        // 숙소 관리자 신청
        <section style={{ backgroundColor: '#0f333f', padding: '24px 0', borderTop: '1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)' }}>
            <div className="container" style={{
                display: 'grid',
                gridTemplateColumns: '1fr auto 1fr', // 💡 좌(비움) : 중앙(문구) : 우(버튼) 3분할
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '16px'
            }}>
                {/* 1. 좌측 균형을 맞추기 위한 빈 영역 */}
                <div />

                {/* 2. 중앙으로 배치되는 문구 영역 */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                    <div style={{
                        width: '36px',
                        height: '36px',
                        borderRadius: '8px',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(110, 231, 183, 0.15)',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center'
                    }}>
                        <Building size={20} color="#6ee7b7" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <strong style={{ display: 'block', color: '#ffffff', fontSize: '15px' }}>
                            숙소를 신청하고 싶은가요?
                        </strong>
                        <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                파트너로 등록하고 더 많은 여행객에게 숙소를 소개해 보세요.
            </span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 3. 우측 끝에 고정되는 버튼 영역 */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(true)}
                        style={{
                            padding: '10px 22px',
                            fontSize: '14px',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            color: '#123f4d',
                            backgroundColor: '#6ee7b7',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderRadius: '24px',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(110, 231, 183, 0.2)',
                        }}
                    >
                        숙소관리자 신청
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* 사업자번호 입력 모달창 */}
            {isModalOpen && (
                <div style={{
                    position: 'fixed',
                    top: 0,
                    left: 0,
                    width: '100vw',
                    height: '100vh',
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55)',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    zIndex: 2000
                }}>
                    <div style={{
                        width: '90%',
                        maxWidth: '400px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '16px',
                        padding: '28px',
                        boxShadow: '0 20px 25px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2)',
                        position: 'relative'
                    }}>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                            style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '18px', right: '18px', background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                        >
                            <X size={20} color="#64748b" />
                        </button>

                        <h3 style={{ margin: '0 0 8px 0', fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a' }}>숙소 관리자(호스트) 신청</h3>
                        <p style={{ margin: '0 0 20px 0', fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                            운영 중이신 사업자등록번호를 입력하시면 즉시 호스트 권한이 부여됩니다.
                        </p>

                        {errorMessage && (
                            <div style={{ padding: '8px 12px', backgroundColor: '#fee2e2', color: '#dc2626', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '12px', marginBottom: '14px' }}>
                                {errorMessage}
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <form onSubmit={handleApply}>
                            <div style={{ marginBottom: '18px' }}>
                                <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#334155', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                    사업자등록번호
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    required
                                    value={businessNo}
                                    onChange={(e) => setBusinessNo(e.target.value)}
                                    placeholder="000-00-00000"
                                    style={{
                                        width: '100%',
                                        padding: '10px 12px',
                                        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                        borderRadius: '8px',
                                        boxSizing: 'border-box',
                                        fontSize: '14px',
                                    }}
                                />
                            </div>

                            <button
                                type="submit"
                                disabled={loading}
                                style={{
                                    width: '100%',
                                    padding: '11px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    fontWeight: 600,
                                    fontSize: '14px',
                                    cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer'
                                }}
                            >
                                {loading ? '신청 진행 중...' : '신청 완료'}
                            </button>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </section>
    );
}

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
//해당 컴포넌트는 예제용으로 차후 삭제 요망
interface ApiResponse {
    status: string;
    message: string;
    port: number;
}

export default function ApiTest() {
    const [data, setData] = useState<ApiResponse | null>(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('/api/hello')
            .then((res) => {
                if (!res.ok) {
                    throw new Error(`HTTP 에러 발생: ${res.status}`);
                }
                return res.json();
            })
            .then((json: ApiResponse) => {
                setData(json);
                setLoading(false);
            })
            .catch((err) => {
                setError(err.message);
                setLoading(false);
            });
    }, []);

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                margin: '20px auto',
                maxWidth: '600px',
                padding: '20px',
                border: '1px solid #4caf50',
                borderRadius: '8px',
                backgroundColor: '#f1f8e9',
                fontFamily: 'sans-serif',
            }}
        >
            <h3 style={{ margin: '0 0 10px 0', color: '#2e7d32' }}>
                🔗 Spring Boot & React 연동 상태
            </h3>

            {loading && <p>데이터를 불러오는 중입니다...</p>}
            {error && <p style={{ color: '#d32f2f' }}>연동 오류: {error}</p>}

            {data && (
                <div style={{ lineHeight: '1.6' }}>
                    <p style={{ margin: '4px 0' }}><strong>상태:</strong> {data.status}</p>
                    <p style={{ margin: '4px 0' }}><strong>서버 메시지:</strong> {data.message}</p>
                    <p style={{ margin: '4px 0' }}><strong>응답 포트:</strong> {data.port}</p>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/hooks/useReservation.ts

import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { createReservation } from '../api/stays';
import type { Booking, Stay } from '../types/stay';

export function useReservation(apiOnline: boolean) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Stay | null>(null);
  const [checkIn, setCheckIn] = useState('');
  const [checkOut, setCheckOut] = useState('');
  const [guests, setGuests] = useState(2);
  const [guestName, setGuestName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.style.overflow = selected ? 'hidden' : '';
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = ''; };
  }, [selected]);

  const open = (stay: Stay) => { setSelected(stay); setFeedback(''); };
  const close = () => { setSelected(null); setFeedback(''); };

  const book = async (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!selected) return;
    if (checkOut <= checkIn) {
      setFeedback('퇴실일은 입실일 다음 날부터 선택해 주세요.');
      return;
    }
    if (!apiOnline) {
      setFeedback('현재 예약 접수를 준비 중입니다.');
      return;
    }
    setSubmitting(true);
    setFeedback('');
    try {
      const booking: Booking = {
        contentId: selected.contentId, // 수정: contentId 바인딩
        checkIn,
        checkOut,
        guests,
        guestName,
        phone
      };
      const data = await createReservation(booking);
      setFeedback(`예약 요청이 접수되었습니다. 예약번호: ${data.reservationCode || data.id}`);
    } catch (error) {
      setFeedback(error instanceof Error ? error.message : '잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return { selected, open, close, checkIn, setCheckIn, checkOut, setCheckOut, guests, setGuests,
    guestName, setGuestName, phone, setPhone, submitting, feedback, book };
}
