File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/hooks/useHeroSlider.ts

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

const slideCount = 3;

export function useHeroSlider() {
  const [slide, setSlide] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setSlide(current => (current + 1) % slideCount), 10000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);
  const previous = () => setSlide(current => (current + slideCount - 1) % slideCount);
  const next = () => setSlide(current => (current + 1) % slideCount);
  return { slide, previous, next };
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/hooks/useStays.ts
========================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { fetchStays } from '../data/stays';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';

export function useStays() {
  const [region, setRegion] = useState('전체 지역');
  const [type, setType] = useState('전체 숙박');
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [term, setTerm] = useState('');
  const [stays, setStays] = useState<Stay[]>([]);
  const [apiOnline, setApiOnline] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // 선택된 지역, 유형, 검색어가 바뀔 때마다 스프링 API 호출
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setLoading(true);

    fetchStays(region, type, term)
        .then(data => {
          if (active) {
            setStays(data);
            setApiOnline(true);
          }
        })
        .catch((err) => {
          console.error('숙소 로딩 실패:', err);
          if (active) setApiOnline(false);
        })
        .finally(() => {
          if (active) setLoading(false);
        });

    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [region, type, term]);

  const selectRegion = (value: string) => {
    setRegion(value);
    document.getElementById('stays')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
  };

  const search = () => {
    setTerm(query);
    document.getElementById('stays')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  return {
    region,
    setRegion,
    type,
    setType,
    query,
    setQuery,
    result: stays,
    loading,
    apiOnline,
    selectRegion,
    search,
  };
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/api/stays.ts
========================================
import type { Booking, BookingResponse, Stay } from '../types/stay';

const apiBase = (import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || '').replace(/\/$/, '');

export async function fetchStays(): Promise<Stay[]> {
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/v1/stays`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('숙소 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
  const stays: unknown = await response.json();
  if (!Array.isArray(stays)) throw new Error('숙소 응답 형식이 올바르지 않습니다.');
  return stays as Stay[];
}

export async function createReservation(booking: Booking): Promise<BookingResponse> {
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/v1/reservations`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(booking),
  });
  const data: BookingResponse = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.message || '예약 요청을 처리하지 못했습니다.');
  return data;
}

import axios from 'axios';

export interface SignupPayload {
    email: string;
    password: string;
    name: string;
    businessNo?: string | null;
}

export interface LoginPayload {
    email: string;
    password: string;
}

export interface TokenResponse {
    token: string;
}

// 회원가입
export const requestSignup = async (payload: SignupPayload): Promise<string> => {
    const response = await axios.post<string>('/api/v1/auth/signup', payload);
    return response.data;
};

// 일반 로그인
export const requestLogin = async (payload: LoginPayload): Promise<TokenResponse> => {
    const response = await axios.post<TokenResponse>('/api/v1/auth/login', payload);
    return response.data;
};

// 카카오 로그인 인증창 URL
const KAKAO_CLIENT_ID = '9c6e519edc47e02f6460edd2afd70d35';
const KAKAO_REDIRECT_URI = 'http://localhost:8080/api/v1/auth/kakao/callback';

export const KAKAO_AUTH_URL = `https://kauth.kakao.com/oauth/authorize?client_id=${KAKAO_CLIENT_ID}&redirect_uri=${KAKAO_REDIRECT_URI}&response_type=code&prompt=login`;
// 숙소 관리자(호스트) 신청 API
export const requestApplyHost = async (businessNo: string): Promise<TokenResponse> => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
    const response = await axios.post<TokenResponse>(
        '/api/v1/auth/apply-host',
        {businessNo},
        {
            headers: {
                Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
            },
        }
    );
    return response.data;
};

import axios from 'axios';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';

// 1. 지역 필터
export const regions = [
 '전체 지역',
 '제주시',
 '애월·한림',
 '함덕·구좌',
 '서귀포·중문',
 '성산·표선',
] as const;

// 2. 숙박 유형 필터
export const types = [
 '전체 숙박',
 '호텔',
 '콘도/리조트',
 '펜션/풀빌라',
 '모텔',
 '캠핑/글램핑',
 '게스트하우스/호스텔',
] as const;

// 3. 메인 추천 여행지 배너
export const destinations = [
 { name: '애월·한림', note: '해안도로와 석양', className: 'spot-a' },
 { name: '서귀포·중문', note: '폭포와 남쪽 바다', className: 'spot-b' },
 { name: '성산·표선', note: '일출과 오름', className: 'spot-c' },
];

// 4. 금액 포맷팅 헬퍼
export const money = (n?: number) => {
 if (!n || n <= 0) return '요금 문의';
 return new Intl.NumberFormat('ko-KR').format(n) + '원';
};

// 5. DB 코드(AC01~AC06)를 한글 숙박 유형명으로 변환
export const getLodgingTypeName = (code?: string): string => {
 switch (code) {
  case 'AC01': return '호텔';
  case 'AC02': return '콘도미니엄';
  case 'AC03': return '펜션/민박';
  case 'AC04': return '모텔';
  case 'AC05': return '캠핑';
  case 'AC06': return '호스텔';
  default: return code || '숙소';
 }
};

// 6. 지역 필터명을 백엔드 검색 키워드로 매핑
export const mapRegionToKeyword = (region: string): string => {
 if (region === '전체 지역' || !region) return '';
 if (region === '애월·한림') return '애월';
 if (region === '함덕·구좌') return '구좌';
 if (region === '서귀포·중문') return '서귀포';
 if (region === '성산·표선') return '성산';
 return region; // '제주시' 등
};

// 7. 숙박 유형명을 백엔드 파라미터(AC01~AC06)로 매핑
export const mapTypeToKeyword = (type: string): string => {
 if (!type || type === '전체 숙박') return '';
 if (type === '호텔') return 'AC01';
 if (type.includes('콘도') || type.includes('리조트')) return 'AC02';
 if (type.includes('펜션')) return 'AC03';
 if (type.includes('모텔')) return 'AC04';
 if (type.includes('캠핑')) return 'AC05';
 if (type.includes('호스텔') || type.includes('게스트하우스')) return 'AC06';
 return type;
};

// 8. 백엔드 숙소 목록 비동기 조회 함수
export const fetchStays = async (
    region?: string,
    type?: string,
    query?: string
): Promise<Stay[]> => {
 const params: Record<string, string> = {};

 const regionKeyword = mapRegionToKeyword(region || '');
 if (regionKeyword) params.region = regionKeyword;

 const typeKeyword = mapTypeToKeyword(type || '');
 if (typeKeyword) params.type = typeKeyword;

 if (query && query.trim()) params.query = query.trim();

 const response = await axios.get<Stay[]>('/api/v1/stays', { params });
 return response.data;
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { requestLogin, KAKAO_AUTH_URL } from '../api/auth';
import axios from 'axios';
import {Footer} from "../components/Footer.tsx";

export function LoginPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');

    const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setErrorMessage('');
        setLoading(true);

        try {
            const data = await requestLogin({ email, password });
            // JWT 토큰 저장
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', data.token);
            alert('로그인되었습니다!');
            navigate('/');
        } catch (err: unknown) {
            if (axios.isAxiosError(err) && err.response) {
                setErrorMessage(err.response.data?.message || '이메일 또는 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
            } else {
                setErrorMessage('서버와 통신할 수 없습니다.');
            }
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    const handleKakaoLogin = () => {
        window.location.href = KAKAO_AUTH_URL;
    };

    return (
        <>
        <div style={{ maxWidth: '420px', margin: '60px auto', padding: '32px', border: '1px solid #e0e0e0', borderRadius: '12px', backgroundColor: '#fff' }}>
            <h2 style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: '8px', color: '#123f4d' }}>로그인</h2>
            <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#666', fontSize: '14px', marginBottom: '24px' }}>제주 야호 서비스를 이용해보세요.</p>

            {/* 카카오 로그인 버튼 */}
            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleKakaoLogin}
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    padding: '12px',
                    backgroundColor: '#FEE500',
                    color: '#000000',
                    border: 'none',
                    borderRadius: '6px',
                    fontWeight: 600,
                    fontSize: '14px',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    gap: '8px',
                    marginBottom: '20px'
                }}
            >
                카카오로 시작하기
            </button>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', margin: '16px 0', color: '#aaa', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#eee' }} />
                <span style={{ padding: '0 10px' }}>또는 이메일로 로그인</span>
                <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#eee' }} />
            </div>

            {errorMessage && (
                <div style={{ padding: '10px 14px', backgroundColor: '#fde8e8', color: '#e02424', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '13px', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                    {errorMessage}
                </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleLogin} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px' }}>
                <div>
                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px' }}>이메일</label>
                    <input
                        type="email"
                        required
                        value={email}
                        onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="example@jeju.com"
                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', border: '1px solid #ccc', borderRadius: '6px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                    />
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px' }}>비밀번호</label>
                    <input
                        type="password"
                        required
                        value={password}
                        onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="비밀번호 입력"
                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', border: '1px solid #ccc', borderRadius: '6px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                    />
                </div>

                <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={loading}
                    style={{
                        marginTop: '10px',
                        padding: '12px',
                        backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                        color: '#fff',
                        border: 'none',
                        borderRadius: '6px',
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                    }}
                >
                    {loading ? '로그인 중...' : '로그인'}
                </button>
            </form>

            <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '20px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#666' }}>
                아직 계정이 없으신가요? <Link to="/signup" style={{ color: '#123f4d', fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: 'none' }}>회원가입</Link>
            </div>
        </div>
            <Footer/>
        </>
    );
}
========================================

========================================
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';

export function KakaoCallback() {
    const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    useEffect(() => {
        const token = searchParams.get('token');
        if (token) {
            // 발급된 JWT 토큰을 로컬스토리지에 저장
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', token);
            alert('카카오 로그인에 성공했습니다!');
            navigate('/'); // 메인 화면으로 이동
        } else {
            alert('로그인에 실패했습니다.');
            navigate('/login');
        }
    }, [searchParams, navigate]);

    return (
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '100px', fontSize: '18px' }}>
            로그인 처리 중입니다. 잠시만 기다려주세요...
        </div>
    );
}

import {useEffect, useState, useMemo} from 'react';
import {useParams, useNavigate} from 'react-router-dom';
import axios from 'axios';
import {
    MapPin,
    Phone,
    Clock,
    Car,
    CheckCircle2,
    ChevronLeft,
    BedDouble,
    User,
    Image as ImageIcon,
    ImageOff,
    X,
    ChevronDown
} from 'lucide-react';
import {money, getLodgingTypeName} from '../data/stays';
import {Footer} from '../components/Footer';
import {KakaoStayMap} from "../components/KakaoMap.tsx";

interface RoomDto {
    roomId: number;
    roomTitle: string;
    roomSize: string;
    roomBaseCount: string;
    roomMaxCount: string;
    pricePerNight: number;
    imageUrls: string[];
}

interface StayDetail {
    contentId: string;
    title: string;
    lodgingType: string;
    tel: string;
    zipcode: string;
    addr: string;
    checkinTime: string;
    checkoutTime: string;
    roomCount: string;
    roomType: string;
    parking: string;
    subFacility: string;
    overview: string;
    firstImage: string;
    firstImage2: string;
    mapX: string;
    mapY: string;
    hostName: string;
    businessNo?: string;
    rooms: RoomDto[];
}


export function StayDetailPage() {
    const {contentId} = useParams<{ contentId: string }>();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [stay, setStay] = useState<StayDetail | null>(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [isGalleryOpen, setIsGalleryOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isSellerInfoOpen, setIsSellerInfoOpen] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        axios.get<StayDetail>(`/api/v1/stays/${contentId}`)
            .then((res) => setStay(res.data))
            .catch((err) => {
                console.error(err);
                alert('숙소 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
                navigate('/');
            })
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, [contentId, navigate]);

    // 모달 활성화 시 배경 스크롤 방지
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isGalleryOpen) {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        } else {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        }
        return () => {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        };
    }, [isGalleryOpen]);

    // 숙소 대표 이미지 + 객실들의 모든 사진을 중복 없이 수집
    const allGalleryImages = useMemo(() => {
        if (!stay) return [];
        const set = new Set<string>();

        if (stay.firstImage) set.add(stay.firstImage);

        if (stay.rooms) {
            stay.rooms.forEach((r) => {
                if (r.imageUrls && r.imageUrls.length > 0) {
                    r.imageUrls.forEach((url) => {
                        if (url) set.add(url);
                    });
                }
            });
        }
        return Array.from(set);
    }, [stay]);

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div style={{textAlign: 'center', padding: '120px 0', color: '#64748b'}}>
                숙소 상세 정보를 불러오는 중입니다...
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (!stay) return null;

    const facilities = stay.subFacility ? stay.subFacility.split(',').map((f) => f.trim()) : [];

    // 상단 5분할 그리드에 노출할 5개 이미지 추출
    const mainImages = allGalleryImages.slice(0, 5);

    return (
        <div style={{backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh'}}>
            {/* 상단 네비게이션 헤더 */}
            <header style={{
                backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                position: 'sticky',
                top: 0,
                zIndex: 100
            }}>
                <div className="container" style={{height: '60px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '16px'}}>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => navigate(-1)}
                        style={{
                            background: 'none',
                            border: 'none',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center'
                        }}
                    >
                        <ChevronLeft size={24} color="#123f4d"/>
                    </button>
                    <span style={{fontSize: '22px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#123f4d'}}>{stay.title}</span>
                </div>
            </header>

            <main className="container" style={{maxWidth: '1050px', margin: '24px auto', padding: '0 16px'}}>
                {/* 1. 상단 5분할 갤러리 섹션 (야놀자 스타일) */}
                <section style={{position: 'relative', marginBottom: '24px'}}>
                    {mainImages.length > 0 ? (
                        <div
                            style={{
                                display: 'grid',
                                gridTemplateColumns: mainImages.length > 1 ? '1.2fr 1fr' : '1fr',
                                gap: '8px',
                                height: '420px',
                                borderRadius: '16px',
                                overflow: 'hidden',
                                backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9'
                            }}
                        >
                            {/* 좌측 메인 대형 사진 */}
                            <div
                                onClick={() => setIsGalleryOpen(true)}
                                style={{
                                    backgroundImage: `url(${mainImages[0]})`,
                                    backgroundSize: 'cover',
                                    backgroundPosition: 'center',
                                    height: '100%',
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    transition: 'opacity 0.2s',
                                }}
                                onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '0.95')}
                                onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '1')}
                            />

                            {/* 우측 2x2 서브 사진 영역 */}
                            {mainImages.length > 1 && (
                                <div
                                    style={{
                                        display: 'grid',
                                        gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr',
                                        gridTemplateRows: '1fr 1fr',
                                        gap: '8px',
                                        height: '100%'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((idx) => {
                                        const imgUrl = mainImages[idx];
                                        return (
                                            <div
                                                key={idx}
                                                onClick={() => setIsGalleryOpen(true)}
                                                style={{
                                                    backgroundImage: imgUrl ? `url(${imgUrl})` : 'none',
                                                    backgroundColor: '#e2e8f0',
                                                    backgroundSize: 'cover',
                                                    backgroundPosition: 'center',
                                                    height: '100%',
                                                    cursor: imgUrl ? 'pointer' : 'default',
                                                    transition: 'opacity 0.2s'
                                                }}
                                                onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                                                    if (imgUrl) e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '0.95';
                                                }}
                                                onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                                                    if (imgUrl) e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '1';
                                                }}
                                            />
                                        );
                                    })}
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <div
                            style={{
                                height: '320px',
                                borderRadius: '16px',

                                justifyContent: 'center',
                                color: '#94a3b8'
                            }}
                        >
                            등록된 이미지가 없습니다.
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {/* 전체 사진 보기 버튼 (오른쪽 하단 플로팅) */}
                    {allGalleryImages.length > 0 && (
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setIsGalleryOpen(true)}
                            style={{
                                position: 'absolute',
                                right: '16px',
                                bottom: '16px',
                                backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95)',
                                color: '#0f172a',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                borderRadius: '24px',
                                padding: '8px 16px',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                fontWeight: 600,
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                gap: '6px',
                                cursor: 'pointer',

                {/* 2. 숙소 헤더 정보 */}

                        alignItems: 'center',

                            maxHeight: '90vh',

                            display: 'flex',
                            flexDirection: 'column',

                        }}

                    >
                        {/* 모달 헤더 */}
                        <div
                            style={{
                                display: 'flex',
                                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                padding: '18px 24px',
                                borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0'
                            }}
                        >
                            <h3 style={{margin: 0, fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a', fontWeight: 700}}>
                                전체 사진 <span
                                style={{color: '#0f766e', fontSize: '15px'}}>({allGalleryImages.length})</span>
                            </h3>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setIsGalleryOpen(false)}
                                style={{
                                    background: 'none',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    padding: '4px',
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    color: '#64748b'
                                }}
                            >
                                <X size={22}/>
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        {/* 모달 이미지 3열 그리드 바디 */}
                        <div
                            style={{

                                overflowY: 'auto',
                                display: 'grid',
                                gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(260px, 1fr))',
                                gap: '14px'
                            }}
                        >
                            {allGalleryImages.map((url, idx) => (
                                <div
                                    key={idx}

                                        width: '100%',

                                        borderRadius: '10px',
                                        overflow: 'hidden',

                                    }}

                                    <img
                                        src={url}
                                        alt={`gallery-${idx}`}
                                        style={{
                                            width: '100%',
                                            height: '100%',
                                            objectFit: 'cover',
                                            display: 'block'
                                        }}
                                        loading="lazy"
                                    />
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}


// 룸 카드 컴포넌트
function RoomCard({ room, stayTitle, stayAddr }: { room: RoomDto; stayTitle: string; stayAddr: string }) {
    const hasImages = room.imageUrls && room.imageUrls.length > 0;
    const [activeImage, setActiveImage] = useState<string>(hasImages ? room.imageUrls[0] : '');
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const handleReserveClick = () => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요한 서비스입니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }
        navigate('/reservation', {
            state: {
                roomId: room.roomId,
                roomTitle: room.roomTitle,
                pricePerNight: room.pricePerNight,
                roomImage: room.imageUrls && room.imageUrls.length > 0 ? room.imageUrls[0] : '',
                stayTitle: stayTitle,
                stayAddr: stayAddr,
            }
        });
    };



    useEffect(() => {
        if (hasImages) {
            setActiveImage(room.imageUrls[0]);
        } else {
            setActiveImage('');
        }
    }, [room.imageUrls, hasImages]);

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                borderRadius: '16px',
                overflow: 'hidden',
                backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04)',
            }}
        >
            {hasImages ? (
                <>
                    <div style={{position: 'relative', width: '100%', height: '280px', backgroundColor: '#ffffff'}}>
                        <img
                            src={activeImage}
                            alt={room.roomTitle}
                            style={{
                                width: '100%',
                                height: '100%',
                                objectFit: 'contain',
                                display: 'block',
                                transition: 'all 0.25s ease',
                            }}
                        />
                        {room.imageUrls.length > 1 && (
                            <div
                                style={{
                                    position: 'absolute',
                                    bottom: '12px',
                                    right: '12px',
                                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65)',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    fontSize: '11px',
                                    padding: '4px 8px',
                                    borderRadius: '12px',
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    gap: '4px',
                                }}
                            >
                                <ImageIcon size={12}/>
                                {room.imageUrls.indexOf(activeImage) + 1} / {room.imageUrls.length}
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    {room.imageUrls.length > 1 && (
                        <div
                            style={{
                                display: 'flex',
                                gap: '8px',
                                padding: '10px 16px',
                                backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                                borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
                                overflowX: 'auto',
                            }}
                        >
                            {room.imageUrls.map((imgUrl, idx) => (
                                <button
                                    key={idx}
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setActiveImage(imgUrl)}
                                    style={{
                                        width: '64px',
                                        height: '48px',
                                        borderRadius: '6px',
                                        overflow: 'hidden',
                                        border: activeImage === imgUrl ? '2px solid #0f766e' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                        padding: 0,
                                        cursor: 'pointer',
                                        flexShrink: 0,
                                        opacity: activeImage === imgUrl ? 1 : 0.65,
                                        transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <img
                                        src={imgUrl}
                                        alt={`thumbnail-${idx}`}
                                        style={{width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover'}}
                                    />
                                </button>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    )}
                </>
            ) : (
                <div
                    style={{
                        width: '100%',
                        height: '180px',
                        backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                        borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        display: 'flex',
                        flexDirection: 'column',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        gap: '8px',
                        color: '#94a3b8',
                    }}
                >
                    <ImageOff size={28} color="#94a3b8"/>
                    <span style={{fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 500}}>
                        객실 사진 정보를 제공하지 않는 숙소입니다.
                    </span>
                </div>
            )}

            <div style={{padding: '20px'}}>
                <div
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'flex-start',
                        flexWrap: 'wrap',
                        gap: '16px',
                    }}
                >
                    <div>
                        <h3 style={{fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 8px 0', fontWeight: 700}}>
                            {room.roomTitle}
                        </h3>
                        <div style={{display: 'flex', gap: '14px', color: '#64748b', fontSize: '13px'}}>
                            <span style={{display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px'}}>
                                <BedDouble size={15} color="#0f766e"/> {room.roomSize ? room.roomSize : '기본 객실'}
                            </span>
                            <span style={{display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px'}}>
                                <User size={15}
                                      color="#0f766e"/> 기준 {room.roomBaseCount || '2'}인 / 최대 {room.roomMaxCount || '4'}인
                            </span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '20px'}}>
                        <div style={{textAlign: 'right'}}>
                            <span style={{fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8', display: 'block'}}>1박 기준</span>
                            <strong style={{fontSize: '22px', color: '#0f766e'}}>{money(room.pricePerNight)}</strong>
                        </div>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={handleReserveClick}
                            style={{
                                padding: '12px 24px',
                                backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                color: '#ffffff',
                                border: 'none',
                                borderRadius: '8px',
                                fontWeight: 600,
                                fontSize: '14px',
                                cursor: 'pointer',
                                transition: 'background-color 0.2s',
                            }}
                            onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#0e313c')}
                            onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#123f4d')}
                        >
                            예약하기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}
========================================
File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/pages/ReservationPage.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import axios from 'axios';
import { Calendar, ChevronLeft, MapPin, User, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import { money } from '../data/stays';
import { Footer } from '../components/Footer';

interface ReservationState {
    roomId: number;
    roomTitle: string;
    pricePerNight: number;
    roomImage: string;
    stayTitle: string;
    stayAddr: string;
}

export function ReservationPage() {
    const location = useLocation();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const roomState = location.state as ReservationState;

    // 오늘 날짜 및 내일 날짜 계산 (기본값)
    const todayStr = useMemo(() => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0], []);
    const tomorrowStr = useMemo(() => {
        const d = new Date();
        d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1);
        return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
    }, []);

    const [checkInDate, setCheckInDate] = useState(todayStr);
    const [checkOutDate, setCheckOutDate] = useState(tomorrowStr);
    const [guestName, setGuestName] = useState('');
    const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
    const [memberId, setMemberId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

    // 전달된 객실 정보가 없으면 뒤로가기
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!roomState || !roomState.roomId) {
            alert('잘못된 접근입니다. 숙소를 먼저 선택해 주세요.');
            navigate(-1);
            return;
        }

        // 토큰에서 사용자 정보 파싱
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요합니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        try {
            const base64Payload = token.split('.')[1];
            const decoded = JSON.parse(
                decodeURIComponent(
                    atob(base64Payload)
                        .split('')
                        .map((c) => '%' + ('00' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16)).slice(-2))
                        .join('')
                )
            );
            if (decoded.name) setGuestName(decoded.name);
            // 백엔드 JWT 토큰 생성 시 memberId가 포함되어 있지 않다면 기본 1로 설정하거나 claim 추가 필요
            setMemberId(decoded.memberId || 1);
        } catch {
            setMemberId(1);
        }
    }, [roomState, navigate]);

    // 박수(Nights) 계산
    const nights = useMemo(() => {
        if (!checkInDate || !checkOutDate) return 0;
        const start = new Date(checkInDate);
        const end = new Date(checkOutDate);
        const diff = (end.getTime() - start.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
        return diff > 0 ? diff : 0;
    }, [checkInDate, checkOutDate]);

    // 총 금액 계산
    const totalPrice = useMemo(() => {
        if (!roomState?.pricePerNight || nights <= 0) return 0;
        return roomState.pricePerNight * nights;
    }, [roomState, nights]);

    // 예약 제출 핸들러
    const handleSubmitReservation = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        if (nights <= 0) {
            alert('체크아웃 날짜는 체크인 날짜 이후여야 합니다.');
            return;
        }

        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인 세션이 만료되었습니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        setSubmitting(true);
        try {
            // 💡 백엔드 ReservationDto.Request 규격 매핑
            const requestBody = {
                memberId: memberId || 1,
                roomId: roomState.roomId,
                checkInDate: checkInDate,
                checkOutDate: checkOutDate,
                totalPrice: totalPrice,
            };

            await axios.post('/api/reservations', requestBody, {
                headers: {
                    Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
                }
            });

            alert('예약이 성공적으로 완료되었습니다!');
            navigate('/');
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error(err);
            const msg = err.response?.data?.message || '예약 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다. (잔여 객실 부족 등)';
            alert(msg);
        } finally {
            setSubmitting(false);
        }
    };

    if (!roomState) return null;

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh' }}>
            <header style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0', position: 'sticky', top: 0, zIndex: 100 }}>
                <div className="container" style={{ height: '60px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '16px' }}>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => navigate(-1)}
                        style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}
                    >
                        <ChevronLeft size={24} color="#123f4d" />
                    </button>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#123f4d' }}>예약 및 결제</span>
                </div>
            </header>

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '960px', margin: '32px auto', padding: '0 16px' }}>
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmitReservation} style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1.4fr 1fr', gap: '24px' }}>

                    {/* 좌측: 예약자 정보 및 일정 선택 */}
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>
                        {/* 1. 일정 선택 카드 */}
                        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '24px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0' }}>
                            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '17px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 16px 0', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                                <Calendar size={18} color="#0f766e" /> 일정 선택
                            </h3>
                            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '14px' }}>
                                <div>
                                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#475569', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                        체크인
                                    </label>
                                    <input
                                        type="date"
                                        min={todayStr}
                                        value={checkInDate}
                                        onChange={(e) => setCheckInDate(e.target.value)}
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', fontSize: '14px' }}
                                        required
                                    />
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#475569', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                        체크아웃
                                    </label>
                                    <input
                                        type="date"
                                        min={checkInDate}
                                        value={checkOutDate}
                                        onChange={(e) => setCheckOutDate(e.target.value)}
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', fontSize: '14px' }}
                                        required
                                    />
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div style={{ marginTop: '12px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                                📅 총 {nights}박 {nights + 1}일 일정
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* 2. 예약자 정보 카드 */}
                        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '24px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0' }}>
                            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '17px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 16px 0', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                                <User size={18} color="#0f766e" /> 예약자 정보
                            </h3>
                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px' }}>
                                <div>
                                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#475569', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                        예약자 성함
                                    </label>
                                    <input
                                        type="text"
                                        value={guestName}
                                        onChange={(e) => setGuestName(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="홍길동"
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', fontSize: '14px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                                        required
                                    />
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#475569', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                        연락처
                                    </label>
                                    <input
                                        type="tel"
                                        value={phone}
                                        onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="010-1234-5678"
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', fontSize: '14px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                                        required
                                    />
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* 우측: 숙소 요약 및 결제 금액 안내 카드 */}
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>
                        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '24px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', boxShadow: '0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.04)' }}>
                            <h4 style={{ fontSize: '14px', color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 700, margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>숙소 정보</h4>
                            <strong style={{ fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a', display: 'block', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                {roomState.stayTitle}
                            </strong>
                            <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px', margin: '0 0 16px 0' }}>
                                <MapPin size={14} /> {roomState.stayAddr}
                            </p>

                            <div style={{ borderTop: '1px solid #f1f5f9', paddingTop: '16px', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '12px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                                    {roomState.roomImage && (
                                        <img
                                            src={roomState.roomImage}
                                            alt={roomState.roomTitle}
                                            style={{ width: '64px', height: '64px', borderRadius: '8px', objectFit: 'cover' }}
                                        />
                                    )}
                                    <div>
                                        <div style={{ fontSize: '15px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a' }}>{roomState.roomTitle}</div>
                                        <div style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b' }}>1박 기준 {money(roomState.pricePerNight)}</div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div style={{ borderTop: '1px solid #f1f5f9', paddingTop: '16px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', color: '#64748b' }}>
                                    <span>객실 요금 ({nights}박)</span>
                                    <span>{money(totalPrice)}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', marginTop: '8px' }}>
                                    <span>최종 결제 금액</span>
                                    <span style={{ color: '#0f766e' }}>{money(totalPrice)}</span>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <button
                                type="submit"
                                disabled={submitting || nights <= 0}
                                style={{
                                    width: '100%',
                                    marginTop: '24px',
                                    padding: '14px 0',
                                    backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    borderRadius: '10px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    fontSize: '15px',
                                    cursor: submitting || nights <= 0 ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                    transition: 'background-color 0.2s',
                                }}
                            >
                                {submitting ? '예약 처리 중...' : `${money(totalPrice)} 예약하기`}
                            </button>

                            <div style={{ marginTop: '12px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '6px', fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
                                <ShieldCheck size={14} color="#0f766e" /> 안전 결제 및 실시간 재고 보장
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </main>

            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
}

File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/pages/SignupPage.tsx

import React, {useState} from 'react';
import {useNavigate, Link} from 'react-router-dom';
import {requestSignup, KAKAO_AUTH_URL} from '../api/auth'; // KAKAO_AUTH_URL 추가
import axios from 'axios';
import {Footer} from "../components/Footer.tsx";

export function SignupPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [passwordConfirm, setPasswordConfirm] = useState('');
    const [name, setName] = useState('');
    const [isHost, setIsHost] = useState(false);
    const [businessNo, setBusinessNo] = useState('');

    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');

    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setErrorMessage('');

        if (password !== passwordConfirm) {
            setErrorMessage('비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
            return;
        }

        if (isHost && !businessNo.trim()) {
            setErrorMessage('호스트 회원은 사업자등록번호를 입력해야 합니다.');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);

        try {
            await requestSignup({
                email,
                password,
                name,
                businessNo: isHost ? businessNo.trim() : null,
            });

            alert('회원가입이 완료되었습니다! 로그인 페이지로 이동합니다.');
            navigate('/login');
        } catch (err: unknown) {
            if (axios.isAxiosError(err) && err.response) {
                setErrorMessage(err.response.data?.message || '회원가입 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
            } else {
                setErrorMessage('서버와 통신할 수 없습니다.');
            }
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    const handleKakaoLogin = () => {
        window.location.href = KAKAO_AUTH_URL;
    };

    return (
        <>
            <div style={{
                maxWidth: '420px',
                margin: '60px auto',
                padding: '32px',
                border: '1px solid #e0e0e0',
                borderRadius: '12px',
                backgroundColor: '#fff'
            }}>
                <h2 style={{textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: '8px', color: '#123f4d'}}>회원가입</h2>
                <p style={{textAlign: 'center', color: '#666', fontSize: '14px', marginBottom: '24px'}}>제주 야호에 오신 것을
                    환영합니다.</p>

                {/* 카카오 간편 회원가입/로그인 버튼 */}
                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleKakaoLogin}
                    style={{
                        width: '100%',
                        padding: '12px',
                        backgroundColor: '#FEE500',
                        color: '#000000',
                        border: 'none',
                        borderRadius: '6px',
                        fontWeight: 600,
                        fontSize: '14px',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        gap: '8px',
                        marginBottom: '20px'
                    }}
                >
                    카카오로 3초 만에 시작하기
                </button>

                <div style={{display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', margin: '16px 0', color: '#aaa', fontSize: '13px'}}>
                    <div style={{flex: 1, height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#eee'}}/>
                    <span style={{padding: '0 10px'}}>또는 이메일로 가입</span>
                    <div style={{flex: 1, height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#eee'}}/>
                </div>

                {errorMessage && (
                    <div style={{
                        padding: '10px 14px',
                        backgroundColor: '#fde8e8',
                        color: '#e02424',
                        borderRadius: '8px',
                        fontSize: '13px',
                        marginBottom: '16px'
                    }}>
                        {errorMessage}
                    </div>
                )}

                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px'}}>
                    <div>
                        <label style={{
                            display: 'block',
                            fontSize: '13px',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            marginBottom: '6px'
                        }}>이메일</label>
                        <input
                            type="email"
                            required
                            value={email}
                            onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="example@jeju.com"
                            style={{
                                width: '100%',
                                padding: '10px 12px',
                                border: '1px solid #ccc',
                                borderRadius: '6px',
                                boxSizing: 'border-box'
                            }}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label style={{
                            display: 'block',
                            fontSize: '13px',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            marginBottom: '6px'
                        }}>비밀번호</label>
                        <input
                            type="password"
                            required
                            value={password}
                            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="비밀번호 입력"
                            style={{
                                width: '100%',
                                padding: '10px 12px',
                                border: '1px solid #ccc',
                                borderRadius: '6px',
                                boxSizing: 'border-box'
                            }}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label style={{display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px'}}>비밀번호
                            확인</label>
                        <input
                            type="password"
                            required
                            value={passwordConfirm}
                            onChange={(e) => setPasswordConfirm(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="비밀번호 재입력"
                            style={{
                                width: '100%',
                                padding: '10px 12px',
                                border: '1px solid #ccc',
                                borderRadius: '6px',
                                boxSizing: 'border-box'
                            }}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label style={{
                            display: 'block',
                            fontSize: '13px',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            marginBottom: '6px'
                        }}>이름</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            required
                            value={name}
                            onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="홍길동"
                            style={{
                                width: '100%',
                                padding: '10px 12px',
                                border: '1px solid #ccc',
                                borderRadius: '6px',
                                boxSizing: 'border-box'
                            }}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div style={{marginTop: '4px'}}>
                        <label style={{
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            gap: '8px',
                            fontSize: '13px',
                            cursor: 'pointer'
                        }}>
                            <input
                                type="checkbox"
                                checked={isHost}
                                onChange={(e) => setIsHost(e.target.checked)}
                            />
                            호스트(숙소 운영자)로 가입하기
                        </label>
                    </div>

                    {isHost && (
                        <div>
                            <label style={{
                                display: 'block',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                fontWeight: 600,
                                marginBottom: '6px'
                            }}>사업자등록번호</label>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                required={isHost}
                                value={businessNo}
                                onChange={(e) => setBusinessNo(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="000-00-00000"
                                style={{
                                    width: '100%',
                                    padding: '10px 12px',
                                    border: '1px solid #ccc',
                                    borderRadius: '6px',
                                    boxSizing: 'border-box'
                                }}
                            />
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <button
                        type="submit"
                        disabled={loading}
                        style={{
                            marginTop: '12px',
                            padding: '12px',
                            backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                            color: '#fff',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderRadius: '6px',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                        }}
                    >
                        {loading ? '가입 처리 중...' : '회원가입 완료'}
                    </button>
                </form>

                <div style={{textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '20px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#666'}}>
                    이미 계정이 있으신가요? <Link to="/login"
                                        style={{color: '#123f4d', fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: 'none'}}>로그인하기</Link>
                </div>
            </div>
            <Footer/>
        </>
    );
}
========================================
File: ./WebContent/hoteol-booking-app/src/pages/HomePage.tsx
